var $=function(id){return document.getElementById(id);};
function money(n){var s=Math.round(Math.abs(n)*100)/100;var t=(s%1?s.toFixed(2):String(s)).replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g,',');return (n<0?'-$':'$')+t;}
function cmoney(n){var a=Math.abs(n),f=function(v,u){var t=v>=100?Math.round(v):Math.round(v*10)/10;return (n<0?'-$':'$')+String(t)+u;};
  if(a<10000)return money(n);if(a<1e6)return f(a/1e3,'K');if(a<1e9)return f(a/1e6,'M');return f(a/1e9,'B');}
function sgn(n){return n>0?'+'+money(n):n<0?'\u2212'+money(-n):money(0);}
function rnd(n){
  try{var a=new Uint32Array(1),lim=Math.floor(4294967296/n)*n;do{crypto.getRandomValues(a);}while(a[0]>=lim);return a[0]%n;}
  catch(e){return Math.floor(Math.random()*n);}
}
function pick(a){return a[Math.floor(Math.random()*a.length)];}
function esc(t){return String(t).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c];});}

/* ---------- pausable timers: nothing runs while the player is in another game ---------- */
var paused=false,timers=[];
function arm(t){t.start=Date.now();t.h=setTimeout(function(){var i=timers.indexOf(t);if(i>=0)timers.splice(i,1);t.fn();},t.left);}
function sched(fn,ms){var t={fn:fn,left:Math.max(0,ms||0),start:0,h:0};timers.push(t);if(!paused)arm(t);return t;}
function unsched(t){if(!t)return;clearTimeout(t.h);var i=timers.indexOf(t);if(i>=0)timers.splice(i,1);}
function later(ms){return new Promise(function(r){sched(r,ms);});}
/* time-based animation loop that freezes while paused */
function tween(dur,step){return new Promise(function(res){var el=0,last=performance.now(),done=false;
  function f(now){if(done)return;var dt=Math.min(50,Math.max(0,now-last));last=now;if(!paused)el+=dt;var k=Math.min(1,el/dur);step(k);if(k>=1){done=true;res();return;}requestAnimationFrame(f);}
  requestAnimationFrame(f);});}
var resumeHooks=[];
function pauseAll(){
  if(paused)return;paused=true;
  timers.forEach(function(t){clearTimeout(t.h);t.left=Math.max(0,t.left-(Date.now()-t.start));});
  try{document.getAnimations().forEach(function(a){if(a.playState==='running'){a.pause();a.__bp=1;}});}catch(e){}
  document.documentElement.classList.add('bpaused');
  try{if(ac&&ac.state==='running')ac.suspend();}catch(e){}
}
function resumeAll(){
  if(!paused)return;paused=false;
  document.documentElement.classList.remove('bpaused');
  try{document.getAnimations().forEach(function(a){if(a.__bp){a.__bp=0;a.play();}});}catch(e){}
  timers.slice().forEach(arm);
  try{if(ac&&ac.state==='suspended'&&unlocked)ac.resume();}catch(e){}
  resumeHooks.forEach(function(f){try{f();}catch(e){}});
}
addEventListener('message',function(e){var d=e.data;if(!d||d.arcade!=='visible')return;if(d.on)resumeAll();else pauseAll();});

/* ---------- sound: WebAudio only, no files ---------- */
var ac=null,unlocked=false,soundOn=true;
function unlock(){unlocked=true;try{var A=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(A&&!ac)ac=new A();if(ac&&ac.state==='suspended'&&!paused)ac.resume();}catch(e){ac=null;}}
document.addEventListener('pointerdown',unlock,true);document.addEventListener('keydown',unlock,true);
function noise(d,vol,delay,hp){if(!soundOn||!unlocked||!ac||paused)return;try{var t=ac.currentTime+(delay||0),len=Math.floor(ac.sampleRate*d),buf=ac.createBuffer(1,len,ac.sampleRate),ch=buf.getChannelData(0);
  for(var i=0;i<len;i++)ch[i]=(Math.random()*2-1)*Math.pow(1-i/len,2);var s=ac.createBufferSource(),g=ac.createGain(),f=ac.createBiquadFilter();f.type='highpass';f.frequency.value=hp||1800;
  s.buffer=buf;g.gain.value=vol||0.15;s.connect(f);f.connect(g);g.connect(ac.destination);s.start(t);}catch(e){}}
function tone(f,d,type,vol,delay,f2){if(!soundOn||!unlocked||!ac||paused)return;try{var t=ac.currentTime+(delay||0),o=ac.createOscillator(),g=ac.createGain();
  o.type=type||'triangle';o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f,t);if(f2)o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(f2,t+d);g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001,t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(vol||0.04,t+0.008);
  g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001,t+d);o.connect(g);g.connect(ac.destination);o.start(t);o.stop(t+d+0.03);}catch(e){}}
var SFX={
  card:function(){noise(0.07,0.12);},
  slide:function(){noise(0.16,0.05,0,900);noise(0.05,0.1,0.13);},
  flip:function(){noise(0.04,0.14,0,2600);},
  chip:function(){tone(1800,0.03,'square',0.02);tone(2400,0.03,'square',0.015,0.03);},
  chips:function(n){for(var i=0;i<(n||4);i++){tone(1700+Math.random()*900,0.025,'square',0.014,i*0.045);}},
  peel:function(){noise(0.09,0.035,0,3200);},
  beat:function(){tone(62,0.16,'sine',0.16);tone(55,0.2,'sine',0.12,0.2);},
  breath:function(){noise(0.5,0.05,0,400);},
  win:function(){[659,784,988].forEach(function(f,i){tone(f,0.12,'triangle',0.05,i*0.07);});},
  natural:function(){[523,659,784,1047,1319].forEach(function(f,i){tone(f,0.16,'triangle',0.05,i*0.07);});tone(1568,0.5,'sine',0.03,0.38);},
  lose:function(){tone(330,0.2,'triangle',0.045);tone(247,0.3,'triangle',0.045,0.15);},
  tie:function(){[784,988,784,988].forEach(function(f,i){tone(f,0.1,'triangle',0.04,i*0.09);});},
  shuffle:function(){for(var i=0;i<14;i++)noise(0.05,0.08,i*0.06);}
};

/* ---------- rules ---------- */
/* house edges computed exactly for an 8-deck shoe (enumerating every coup) */
var R={comm:'std',tie:8,cine:true,squeeze:false,shufCine:true};
var pendingR=null;
var EDGE={player:1.24,std:1.06,nc:1.46,ez:1.02,tie8:14.36,tie9:4.84};
function bankerEdge(){return EDGE[R.comm];}
function tieEdge(){return R.tie===9?EDGE.tie9:EDGE.tie8;}
function commName(){return R.comm==='std'?'5% commission':R.comm==='nc'?'No-commission':'EZ Baccarat';}
function rulesText(){
  $('tiePay').textContent='pays '+R.tie+' to 1';
  $('bankPay').textContent=R.comm==='std'?'pays 0.95 to 1':R.comm==='nc'?'1 to 1 \u00b7 6 pays 1:2':'1 to 1 \u00b7 3-card 7 bars';
  $('commTxt').textContent=R.comm==='std'?'5% commission':R.comm==='nc'?'No commission':'EZ Baccarat';
  $('edge').innerHTML='<b>'+commName()+'</b>: Banker edge <b>'+bankerEdge().toFixed(2)+'%</b>, Player <b>'+EDGE.player.toFixed(2)+'%</b>, Tie at '+R.tie+' to 1 <b>'+tieEdge().toFixed(2)+'%</b>. '+
    (R.comm==='std'?'Banker wins pay 19 to 20 after the 5% commission.':R.comm==='nc'?'Banker wins pay even money, except a Banker win on 6 pays half.':'Banker wins pay even money, except a Banker win with a three-card 7 (the Dragon) is a push.')+
    ' Player and Banker bets push on a tie. Eight decks, exact third-card rules. Changes made during a hand start with the next one.';
}

/* ---------- bets: the main layout plus side bets ---------- */
function bv(c){return c.r>=10?0:c.r;}
function tot(cs){var t=0;cs.forEach(function(c){t+=bv(c);});return t%10;}
var MAIN={
  player:{id:'player',name:'Player',short:'Player',tag:'P'},
  banker:{id:'banker',name:'Banker',short:'Banker',tag:'B'},
  tie:{id:'tie',name:'Tie',short:'Tie',tag:'T'}
};
var DRAGON=[['Natural win','1 to 1'],['Natural tie','Push'],['Win by 9','30 to 1'],['Win by 8','10 to 1'],['Win by 7','6 to 1'],['Win by 6','4 to 1'],['Win by 5','2 to 1'],['Win by 4','1 to 1']];
function dragonEval(x,side){
  var me=side==='P'?x.pt:x.bt,op=side==='P'?x.bt:x.pt,nat=side==='P'?x.pnat:x.bnat;
  if(me>op){if(nat)return [1,'Natural win'];var m=me-op,p={9:30,8:10,7:6,6:4,5:2,4:1}[m];return p?[p,'Win by '+m]:null;}
  if(me===op&&(x.pnat||x.bnat))return 'push';return null;
}
var SIDES=[
  {id:'pp',name:'Player Pair',short:'P Pair',he:'10.36%',about:'Wins when the Player\u2019s first two cards are the same rank.',table:[['Player pair','11 to 1']],
   eval:function(x){return x.p[0].r===x.p[1].r?[11,'Player pair']:null;}},
  {id:'bp',name:'Banker Pair',short:'B Pair',he:'10.36%',about:'Wins when the Banker\u2019s first two cards are the same rank.',table:[['Banker pair','11 to 1']],
   eval:function(x){return x.b[0].r===x.b[1].r?[11,'Banker pair']:null;}},
  {id:'ep',name:'Either Pair',short:'Either',he:'13.71%',about:'Wins when either the Player or the Banker starts with a pair.',table:[['Player or Banker pair','5 to 1']],
   eval:function(x){return (x.p[0].r===x.p[1].r||x.b[0].r===x.b[1].r)?[5,'Pair']:null;}},
  {id:'perf',name:'Perfect Pair',short:'Perfect',he:'8.05%',about:'Wins when the Player or the Banker starts with a suited pair (same rank and suit).',table:[['Both hands suited pairs','200 to 1'],['One suited pair','25 to 1']],
   eval:function(x){var a=x.p[0].r===x.p[1].r&&x.p[0].s===x.p[1].s,b=x.b[0].r===x.b[1].r&&x.b[0].s===x.b[1].s;return a&&b?[200,'Both hands suited pairs']:(a||b)?[25,'One suited pair']:null;}},
  {id:'dbp',name:'Dragon Bonus Player',short:'Dragon P',he:'2.65%',about:'Bets on the Player winning big. Pays by the winning margin, or even money on a natural win.',table:DRAGON,
   eval:function(x){return dragonEval(x,'P');}},
  {id:'dbb',name:'Dragon Bonus Banker',short:'Dragon B',he:'9.37%',about:'Bets on the Banker winning big. Pays by the winning margin, or even money on a natural win.',table:DRAGON,
   eval:function(x){return dragonEval(x,'B');}},
  {id:'panda',name:'Panda 8',short:'Panda 8',he:'10.19%',ez:true,about:'EZ Baccarat: Player wins with a three-card total of 8.',table:[['Player wins with a 3-card 8','25 to 1']],
   eval:function(x){return x.w==='P'&&x.p.length===3&&x.pt===8?[25,'Panda 8']:null;}},
  {id:'dragon',name:'Dragon 7',short:'Dragon 7',he:'7.61%',ez:true,about:'EZ Baccarat: Banker wins with a three-card total of 7.',table:[['Banker wins with a 3-card 7','40 to 1']],
   eval:function(x){return x.w==='B'&&x.b.length===3&&x.bt===7?[40,'Dragon 7']:null;}},
  {id:'big',name:'Big',short:'Big',he:'4.35%',about:'Wins when the coup uses 5 or 6 cards (someone draws).',table:[['5 or 6 cards dealt','0.54 to 1']],
   eval:function(x){return x.n>=5?[0.54,x.n+' cards']:null;}},
  {id:'small',name:'Small',short:'Small',he:'5.28%',about:'Wins when the coup uses only 4 cards (both hands stand).',table:[['4 cards dealt','1.5 to 1']],
   eval:function(x){return x.n===4?[1.5,'4 cards']:null;}}
];
function sideDef(id){for(var i=0;i<SIDES.length;i++)if(SIDES[i].id===id)return SIDES[i];return null;}
function sideAllowed(d){return !d.ez||R.comm==='ez';}
function betName(id){return MAIN[id]?MAIN[id].name:(sideDef(id)||{name:id}).name;}
function fmtPay(v){return typeof v==='string'?v:(v+' to 1');}
/* resolve one bet: returns {k:'win'|'lose'|'push', profit, label} */
function settleBet(id,amt,x){
  if(id==='player'){if(x.w==='P')return {k:'win',profit:amt,label:'Player wins'};if(x.w==='T')return {k:'push',profit:0,label:'Push'};return {k:'lose',profit:-amt};}
  if(id==='banker'){if(x.w==='T')return {k:'push',profit:0,label:'Push'};if(x.w!=='B')return {k:'lose',profit:-amt};
    if(R.comm==='std'){var c=Math.round(amt*0.05*100)/100;return {k:'win',profit:amt-c,label:'Banker wins',comm:c};}
    if(R.comm==='nc')return x.bt===6?{k:'win',profit:amt/2,label:'Banker 6 pays half'}:{k:'win',profit:amt,label:'Banker wins'};
    if(x.b.length===3&&x.bt===7)return {k:'push',profit:0,label:'Dragon 7 bars the Banker'};
    return {k:'win',profit:amt,label:'Banker wins'};}
  if(id==='tie'){return x.w==='T'?{k:'win',profit:amt*R.tie,label:'Tie pays '+R.tie+' to 1'}:{k:'lose',profit:-amt};}
  var d=sideDef(id);if(!d)return {k:'lose',profit:-amt};var r=d.eval(x);
  if(r==='push')return {k:'push',profit:0,label:'Natural tie'};
  if(r)return {k:'win',profit:Math.round(amt*r[0]*100)/100,label:r[1],mult:r[0]};
  return {k:'lose',profit:-amt};
}

/* ---------- the coup: exact Punto Banco tableau ---------- */
function bankerDraws(b,p3){
  if(p3===null)return b<=5;
  if(b<=2)return true;
  if(b===3)return p3!==8;
  if(b===4)return p3>=2&&p3<=7;
  if(b===5)return p3>=4&&p3<=7;
  if(b===6)return p3===6||p3===7;
  return false;
}
function coupCtx(p,b){
  var pt=tot(p),bt=tot(b),p2=tot(p.slice(0,2)),b2=tot(b.slice(0,2));
  var x={p:p,b:b,pt:pt,bt:bt,n:p.length+b.length,pnat:p.length===2&&p2>=8,bnat:b.length===2&&b2>=8,w:pt>bt?'P':bt>pt?'B':'T'};
  x.pp=p[0].r===p[1].r;x.bp=b[0].r===b[1].r;x.nat=(p2>=8||b2>=8);return x;
}
/* plays a whole coup from a list of cards in dealing order (used by tests) */
function playCoup(cards){
  var i=0,p=[cards[i++]],b=[cards[i++]];p.push(cards[i++]);b.push(cards[i++]);
  var pt=tot(p),bt=tot(b);
  if(pt<8&&bt<8){var p3=null;if(pt<=5){p.push(cards[i++]);p3=bv(p[2]);}if(bankerDraws(bt,p3))b.push(cards[i++]);}
  return coupCtx(p,b);
}

/* ---------- shoe: 8 decks, cut card, burn ---------- */
var DECKS=8;
var shoe=[],cutAt=0,discards=0,totalCards=0,cutReached=false,cutPopped=false,nextId=1,shoesPlayed=1,needShuffle=true,lastHandOfShoe=false;
function prepareShoe(){
  shoe=[];var S=['s','h','d','c'];
  for(var d=0;d<DECKS;d++)for(var si=0;si<4;si++)for(var r=1;r<=13;r++)shoe.push({s:S[si],r:r,id:nextId++});
  for(var i=shoe.length-1;i>0;i--){var j=rnd(i+1),t=shoe[i];shoe[i]=shoe[j];shoe[j]=t;}
  totalCards=shoe.length;discards=0;cutReached=false;cutPopped=false;lastHandOfShoe=false;
}
/* frac = where the player's cut card went (0..1 from the front); the cards in front go to the back.
   In baccarat the reshuffle card goes about 16 cards from the end of the shoe. */
function finishShoe(frac,left){
  var n=shoe.length,k=Math.max(1,Math.min(n-1,Math.round(frac*n)));
  shoe=shoe.slice(n-k).concat(shoe.slice(0,n-k));
  cutAt=left;shoeHud();
}
function burnCount(c){return c.r>=10?10:c.r;}
/* baccarat burn: turn the first card face up, then burn that many more face down */
function doBurn(){var c=shoe.pop();var n=burnCount(c);for(var i=0;i<n&&shoe.length;i++)shoe.pop();discards+=1+n;shoeHud();return {card:c,n:n};}
function buildShoeQuick(){prepareShoe();finishShoe(0.3+rnd(41)/100,14+rnd(5));var b=doBurn();needShuffle=false;return b;}
function drawCard(){
  if(!shoe.length){buildShoeQuick();toast('Shoe ran dry: reshuffled mid-coup');}
  var c=shoe.pop();
  if(shoe.length<=cutAt&&!cutReached){cutReached=true;lastHandOfShoe=true;toast('Cut card is out: one more coup after this, then a fresh shoe');}
  shoeHud();return c;
}
function shoeHud(){
  var left=shoe.length,frac=totalCards?left/totalCards:0;
  $('shoeN').textContent=totalCards?left+' cards \u00b7 '+(left/52).toFixed(1)+' decks':'';
  $('shoeFill').style.width=(frac*100)+'%';$('shoeCut').style.left=(totalCards?cutAt/totalCards*100:0)+'%';
  $('stackv').style.width='calc(var(--cw)*'+(0.08+frac*0.5).toFixed(3)+')';
  $('cutMark').style.right='calc(10% + var(--cw)*'+(totalCards?cutAt/totalCards*0.5:0).toFixed(3)+')';
  $('weight').style.right='calc(4% + var(--cw)*'+(0.02+frac*0.5).toFixed(3)+' + 2px)';
  if(cutReached&&!cutPopped){cutPopped=true;var cm=$('cutMark');
    if(cm.animate&&cm.style.display!=='none'){cm.animate([{transform:'none',opacity:1},{transform:'translate(-6px,-22px) rotate(-28deg)',opacity:0}],{duration:650,easing:'cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.3,1)',fill:'forwards'}).onfinish=function(){cm.style.display='none';};}
    else cm.style.display='none';}
  if(!cutReached){var cm2=$('cutMark');cm2.style.display='block';if(cm2.getAnimations)cm2.getAnimations().forEach(function(a){a.cancel();});}
  $('shoePen').textContent=totalCards?(cutReached?'Cut card is out':Math.max(0,left-cutAt)+' to the cut card'):'';
  $('trayN').textContent=discards+' card'+(discards===1?'':'s');
  $('trayFill').style.height=(totalCards?Math.min(92,discards/totalCards*100*1.1):0)+'%';
}

/* ---------- cinematic shuffle: wash, riffle, your cut, cut card, load the shoe, baccarat burn ---------- */
var Ceremony=(function(){
  var ov,stage,ctl,skipped=false,waiters=[];
  function wait(ms){return new Promise(function(res){if(skipped){res();return;}var t=sched(done,ms);function done(){unsched(t);var i=waiters.indexOf(done);if(i>=0)waiters.splice(i,1);res();}waiters.push(done);});}
  function skip(){skipped=true;waiters.slice().forEach(function(f){f();});}
  function cap(t,sub){$('shufTitle').textContent=t;$('shufSub').innerHTML=sub||'';}
  function backCard(){var el=makeCard({s:'s',r:2,id:0},true);el.removeAttribute('data-id');el.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');return el;}
  function put(el,x,y,r,dur,delay,ease){
    var to='translate('+x.toFixed(1)+'px,'+y.toFixed(1)+'px) rotate('+(r||0).toFixed(1)+'deg)',from=el._t||to;el._t=to;el.style.transform=to;
    if(!dur||skipped||!el.animate)return;
    el.animate([{transform:from},{transform:to}],{duration:dur,delay:delay||0,easing:ease||'cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.3,1)',fill:'backwards'});
  }
  function rel(el){var a=el.getBoundingClientRect(),b=stage.getBoundingClientRect();return {x:a.left+a.width/2-(b.left+b.width/2),y:a.top+a.height/2-(b.top+b.height/2),w:a.width,h:a.height};}
  function tick(delay){noise(0.014,0.07,delay/1000);}
  async function wash(cards){
    cap('The wash','Eight fresh decks spread face down and mixed by hand, the way the salon does it');
    var W=Math.min(stage.clientWidth*0.42,230),H=Math.min(stage.clientHeight*0.36,80);
    for(var pass=0;pass<3&&!skipped;pass++){
      cards.forEach(function(el,i){var a=Math.random()*Math.PI*2,rr=0.35+Math.random()*0.65;put(el,Math.cos(a)*W*rr,Math.sin(a)*H*rr,rnd(360)-180,640,i*7,'cubic-bezier(.4,.1,.3,1)');if(i%4===0)tick(i*7);});
      noise(0.5,0.035,0.05,600);
      await wait(660+cards.length*7);
    }
    cards.forEach(function(el,i){put(el,(rnd(9)-4),-i*0.8,(rnd(7)-3),520,i*12);});
    await wait(cards.length*12+540);
  }
  async function riffle(cards,n,of){
    cap('Riffle '+n+' of '+of,'Splitting the stack and weaving the two halves together');
    var half=Math.floor(cards.length/2),L=cards.slice(0,half),Rr=cards.slice(half),sep=Math.min(stage.clientWidth*0.26,150);
    L.forEach(function(el,i){put(el,-sep,-i*0.8,-7,320,i*6);});
    Rr.forEach(function(el,i){put(el,sep,-i*0.8,7,320,i*6);});
    await wait(420);
    L.forEach(function(el,i){put(el,-sep+8,-i*0.8-6,-16,160);});Rr.forEach(function(el,i){put(el,sep-8,-i*0.8-6,16,160);});
    await wait(180);
    var order=[],li=0,ri=0,side=rnd(2);
    while(li<L.length||ri<Rr.length){var run=1+rnd(3);
      for(var k=0;k<run;k++){if(side===0&&li<L.length)order.push(L[li++]);else if(side===1&&ri<Rr.length)order.push(Rr[ri++]);}
      side=1-side;}
    order.forEach(function(el,k){var d=k*34;sched(function(){el.style.zIndex=10+k;},d);put(el,(rnd(5)-2),-k*0.8,(rnd(5)-2)*0.6,170,d,'ease-out');tick(d);});
    await wait(order.length*34+220);
    order.forEach(function(el,k){put(el,0,-k*0.8,0,180,0);});
    noise(0.12,0.08);await wait(260);
    return order;
  }
  function askCut(brick){
    return new Promise(function(resolve){
      var frac=0.5,cc=document.createElement('div'),tag=document.createElement('div');
      cc.className='ccard pulse';tag.className='ccTag';brick.appendChild(cc);brick.appendChild(tag);
      function show(){frac=Math.max(0.1,Math.min(0.9,frac));cc.style.left=(frac*100)+'%';tag.style.left=(frac*100)+'%';tag.textContent=Math.round(frac*totalCards)+' cards in front';}
      show();
      var dragging=false;
      function fromEvent(e){var r=brick.getBoundingClientRect();frac=(e.clientX-r.left)/r.width;show();}
      function down(e){dragging=true;try{brick.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_){}fromEvent(e);e.preventDefault();}
      function move(e){if(dragging||e.pointerType==='mouse')fromEvent(e);}
      function up(){dragging=false;}
      brick.addEventListener('pointerdown',down);brick.addEventListener('pointermove',move);brick.addEventListener('pointerup',up);brick.addEventListener('pointercancel',up);
      ctl.innerHTML='';
      var go=document.createElement('button');go.className='btn';go.type='button';go.textContent='Cut here';
      var deal=document.createElement('button');deal.className='btn2';deal.type='button';deal.textContent='Let the dealer cut';
      ctl.appendChild(deal);ctl.appendChild(go);
      function finish(f){document.removeEventListener('keydown',key,true);brick.removeEventListener('pointerdown',down);brick.removeEventListener('pointermove',move);
        cc.classList.remove('pulse');ctl.innerHTML='';var wi=waiters.indexOf(onSkip);if(wi>=0)waiters.splice(wi,1);resolve({frac:f,cc:cc,tag:tag});}
      function key(e){if(e.key==='ArrowLeft'){frac-=0.02;show();e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();}
        else if(e.key==='ArrowRight'){frac+=0.02;show();e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();}
        else if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();finish(frac);}}
      document.addEventListener('keydown',key,true);
      go.addEventListener('click',function(){finish(frac);});
      deal.addEventListener('click',function(){frac=0.3+rnd(41)/100;show();finish(frac);});
      function onSkip(){frac=0.3+rnd(41)/100;finish(frac);}
      if(skipped){onSkip();return;}
      waiters.push(onSkip);
      setTimeout(function(){try{go.focus({preventScroll:true});}catch(e){}},60);
    });
  }
  async function run(first){
    ov=$('shuf');stage=$('shufStage');ctl=$('shufCtl');skipped=false;waiters=[];stage.innerHTML='';ctl.innerHTML='';
    $('shufSkip').onclick=skip;
    ov.classList.remove('see');ov.classList.add('on');SFX.shuffle();
    cap(first?'Fresh shoe':'Shuffling up',first?'Opening eight new decks':'Collecting the discards and what is left in the shoe');
    var N=42,cards=[],src=[rel($('shoe')),rel($('trayBin'))];
    for(var i=0;i<N;i++){var el=backCard();stage.appendChild(el);el.style.zIndex=10+i;var p=src[i%2];el._t='translate('+p.x+'px,'+p.y+'px) rotate('+(rnd(30)-15)+'deg)';el.style.transform=el._t;cards.push(el);}
    $('stackv').style.width='calc(var(--cw)*0.02)';$('trayFill').style.height='0%';$('trayN').textContent='0 cards';$('shoeN').textContent='Shuffling';$('shoePen').textContent='';$('cutMark').style.display='none';
    await wait(60);
    cards.forEach(function(el,i){put(el,(rnd(9)-4),-i*0.8,(rnd(7)-3),520,i*14);if(i%3===0)tick(i*14);});
    await wait(N*14+560);
    if(!skipped)await wash(cards);
    for(var r=1;r<=2&&!skipped;r++)cards=await riffle(cards,r,2);
    prepareShoe();
    stage.innerHTML='';
    var bw=Math.min(stage.clientWidth-30,430),brick=document.createElement('div');brick.className='brick';brick.style.width=bw+'px';
    var seg=document.createElement('div');seg.className='bseg';seg.style.left='0';seg.style.width='100%';brick.appendChild(seg);
    for(var d=1;d<DECKS;d++){var ln=document.createElement('div');ln.className='deckLine';ln.style.left=(d/DECKS*100)+'%';brick.appendChild(ln);}
    brick.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<span class="end l">Front &middot; dealt first</span><span class="end r">Back</span>');
    stage.appendChild(brick);
    if(brick.animate)brick.animate([{opacity:0,transform:'translate(-50%,-50%) scale(.6)'},{opacity:1,transform:'translate(-50%,-50%)'}],{duration:350,easing:'cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.3,1)'});
    cap('Your cut','Drag or tap to slide the <em>yellow cut card</em> into the stack, then press <em>Cut here</em>.');
    var res=await askCut(brick),f=res.frac;
    cap('Cutting the deck','The '+Math.round(f*totalCards)+' cards in front of your cut card move to the back');
    seg.remove();var A=document.createElement('div'),B=document.createElement('div');A.className='bseg';B.className='bseg';
    A.style.left='0';A.style.width=(f*100)+'%';B.style.left=(f*100)+'%';B.style.width=((1-f)*100)+'%';brick.insertBefore(B,brick.firstChild);brick.insertBefore(A,brick.firstChild);
    res.cc.style.opacity='0';res.tag.style.opacity='0';brick.querySelectorAll('.deckLine').forEach(function(x){x.remove();});
    if(!skipped&&A.animate){
      noise(0.2,0.06);
      A.animate([{transform:'none'},{transform:'translateY(-120%)'}],{duration:320,easing:'ease-out',fill:'forwards'});await wait(330);
      B.animate([{transform:'none'},{transform:'translateX('+(-f*bw)+'px)'}],{duration:340,easing:'ease-in-out',fill:'forwards'});
      A.animate([{transform:'translateY(-120%)'},{transform:'translate('+((1-f)*bw)+'px,-120%)'}],{duration:340,easing:'ease-in-out',fill:'forwards'});await wait(360);
      A.animate([{transform:'translate('+((1-f)*bw)+'px,-120%)'},{transform:'translate('+((1-f)*bw)+'px,0)'}],{duration:260,easing:'ease-in',fill:'forwards'});noise(0.1,0.08,0.22);await wait(320);
    }
    var left=14+rnd(5),pen=1-left/totalCards;
    var rc=document.createElement('div'),rt=document.createElement('div');rc.className='ccard red';rt.className='ccTag red';rc.style.left=(pen*100)+'%';rt.style.left=(pen*100)+'%';
    rt.textContent='Cut card \u00b7 '+left+' cards from the end';brick.appendChild(rc);brick.appendChild(rt);
    cap('Placing the cut card','When it comes out, one more coup is dealt and then the shoe is done');
    if(!skipped&&rc.animate){rc.animate([{transform:'translateY(-90px)',opacity:0},{transform:'none',opacity:1}],{duration:420,easing:'cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.3,1)'});tone(1200,0.05,'square',0.02,0.35);}
    await wait(1100);
    cap('Loading the shoe','');ov.classList.add('see');
    var target=rel($('shoe')),br=rel(brick);
    if(!skipped&&brick.animate){
      var sc=Math.max(0.15,target.w/Math.max(1,br.w));
      brick.animate([{transform:'translate(-50%,-50%)'},{transform:'translate(calc(-50% + '+target.x+'px),calc(-50% + '+target.y+'px)) scale('+sc+')',opacity:.2}],{duration:620,easing:'cubic-bezier(.5,0,.3,1)',fill:'forwards'});
      noise(0.3,0.05,0.4);
    }
    await wait(640);stage.innerHTML='';
    finishShoe(f,left);
    /* the burn: first card face up, then that many face down */
    ov.classList.remove('see');
    var b=doBurn();
    cap('Burn card','The dealer turns the first card: <em>'+RANK_TXT[b.card.r]+'</em> means <em>'+b.n+'</em> more card'+(b.n>1?'s':'')+' go to the discard tray face down');
    var bcw=document.createElement('div');bcw.className='burnCard';var bce=makeCard(b.card,true);bcw.appendChild(bce);stage.appendChild(bcw);
    var bt=document.createElement('div');bt.className='burnTag';bt.textContent='Burn '+b.n;stage.appendChild(bt);bt.style.opacity='0';
    if(!skipped&&bcw.animate){var sr=rel($('shoe'));bcw.animate([{transform:'translate('+sr.x+'px,'+sr.y+'px) scale(.5)',opacity:.4},{transform:'none',opacity:1}],{duration:480,easing:'cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.3,1)'});SFX.slide();}
    await wait(560);bce.classList.remove('down');SFX.flip();await wait(420);bt.style.opacity='1';
    if(!skipped){
      var tr=rel($('trayBin'));
      for(var k=0;k<b.n&&!skipped;k++){var fc=backCard();fc.style.zIndex=30+k;stage.appendChild(fc);fc._t='translate(0px,0px)';
        var ex=tr.x+(rnd(9)-4),ey=tr.y;put(fc,ex,ey,rnd(40)-20,0);
        if(fc.animate)fc.animate([{transform:'translate(60px,-10px) rotate(0deg)',opacity:1},{transform:'translate('+ex+'px,'+ey+'px) rotate(12deg) scale(.6)',opacity:.15}],{duration:460,easing:'cubic-bezier(.3,.7,.3,1)',fill:'forwards'});
        SFX.card();await wait(110);}
      await wait(500);
    }
    stage.innerHTML='';shoeHud();
    ov.classList.remove('on','see');ctl.innerHTML='';
    toast('Shoe ready \u00b7 '+b.n+' burned \u00b7 cut card '+left+' from the end');
    return b;
  }
  return {run:run,skip:skip,active:function(){return $('shuf').classList.contains('on');}};
})();

/* ---------- scoreboard: bead plate, big road and the three derived roads ---------- */
var history=[];
var COL={P:'#3b82f6',B:'#ef4444',T:'#10b981',R:'#ef4444',Bl:'#3b82f6'};
function layoutRoad(seq){
  /* seq: [{w:'X',...}] without ties. Streaks go down six rows, then tail right (the dragon tail). */
  var occ={},cells=[],cols=[],last=null,startCol=-1,pos=null,tail=false;
  seq.forEach(function(h){
    if(last===null||h.w!==last){
      var c=startCol+1;while(occ[c+',0'])c++;startCol=c;pos={c:c,r:0};tail=false;cols.push(0);
    } else {
      var r=pos.r+1;
      if(!tail&&r<6&&!occ[pos.c+','+r])pos={c:pos.c,r:r};
      else{tail=true;var c2=pos.c+1;while(occ[c2+','+pos.r])c2++;pos={c:c2,r:pos.r};}
    }
    occ[pos.c+','+pos.r]=1;var cell={c:pos.c,r:pos.r,h:h};cells.push(cell);cols[cols.length-1]++;last=h.w;
  });
  return {cells:cells,cols:cols};
}
function bigRoadOf(hist){
  var seq=[],lead=0;
  hist.forEach(function(h){if(h.w==='T'){if(seq.length)seq[seq.length-1].ties++;else lead++;}else seq.push({w:h.w,ties:0,pp:h.pp,bp:h.bp,nat:h.nat});});
  var L=layoutRoad(seq);L.lead=lead;return L;
}
function derivedMarks(cols,k){
  var out=[];
  for(var ci=0;ci<cols.length;ci++)for(var ri=0;ri<cols[ci];ri++){
    if(ci<k||(ci===k&&ri===0))continue;
    if(ri===0)out.push(cols[ci-1]===cols[ci-1-k]?'R':'Bl');
    else{var L=cols[ci-k];out.push((L>=ri+1||L<ri)?'R':'Bl');}
  }
  return out;
}
function askMarks(next){
  var h=history.concat([{w:next}]),L=bigRoadOf(h),o=[];
  [1,2,3].forEach(function(k){var m=derivedMarks(L.cols,k),before=derivedMarks(bigRoadOf(history).cols,k);o.push(m.length>before.length?m[m.length-1]:null);});
  return o;
}
var SVGNS='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
function svgEl(tag,a){var e=document.createElementNS(SVGNS,tag);for(var k in a)e.setAttribute(k,a[k]);return e;}
function gridSvg(box,cell,rows,maxCol){
  var w=box.clientWidth||300,vis=Math.max(4,Math.floor((w-2)/cell)),off=Math.max(0,maxCol+2-vis);
  var W=vis*cell,H=rows*cell,s=svgEl('svg',{viewBox:'0 0 '+W+' '+H,width:W,height:H,'aria-hidden':'true'});s.style.width=W+'px';s.style.height=H+'px';
  var g='';for(var i=1;i<vis;i++)g+='M'+(i*cell)+' 0V'+H;for(var j=1;j<rows;j++)g+='M0 '+(j*cell)+'H'+W;
  s.appendChild(svgEl('path',{d:g,stroke:'rgba(148,163,184,.13)','stroke-width':'1',fill:'none'}));
  var old=box.querySelector('svg');if(old)old.remove();box.insertBefore(s,box.firstChild);
  return {s:s,off:off,vis:vis};
}
function pairDots(s,x,y,cell,h){
  var rr=Math.max(1.6,cell*.13);
  if(h.bp)s.appendChild(svgEl('circle',{cx:x+cell*.2,cy:y+cell*.2,r:rr,fill:COL.B,stroke:'#fff','stroke-width':.8}));
  if(h.pp)s.appendChild(svgEl('circle',{cx:x+cell*.8,cy:y+cell*.8,r:rr,fill:COL.P,stroke:'#fff','stroke-width':.8}));
}
var roadSizes={big:13};
function drawRoads(){
  var felt=$('felt');if(!felt)return;
  var small=felt.classList.contains('small'),fsShort=document.documentElement.classList.contains('fsshort');
  var H=document.documentElement,vh=window.__fsvh||innerHeight;var bigBox=$('rBig');var fsN=H.classList.contains('fsnarrow'),side=H.classList.contains('fs')&&!fsN&&!fsShort;
  var s=roadSizes.big=fsN?(vh>=900?13:vh>=800?12:11):side?Math.max(12,Math.min(22,Math.floor(((bigBox.clientWidth||300)-2)/24))):H.classList.contains('fs')?Math.round(Math.max(9,Math.min(14,(vh-260)/48))):small?11:13;
  /* big road */
  var BR=bigRoadOf(history),maxC=0;BR.cells.forEach(function(c){maxC=Math.max(maxC,c.c);});
  var G=gridSvg(bigBox,s,6,maxC);
  if(!BR.cells.length&&BR.lead){G.s.appendChild(svgEl('line',{x1:s*.2,y1:s*.8,x2:s*.8,y2:s*.2,stroke:COL.T,'stroke-width':2}));}
  BR.cells.forEach(function(c,i){var x=(c.c-G.off)*s,y=c.r*s;if(x<0)return;
    G.s.appendChild(svgEl('circle',{cx:x+s/2,cy:y+s/2,r:s*.36,fill:'none',stroke:COL[c.h.w],'stroke-width':Math.max(1.6,s*.14)}));
    var ties=c.h.ties+(i===0?BR.lead:0);
    if(ties){G.s.appendChild(svgEl('line',{x1:x+s*.18,y1:y+s*.82,x2:x+s*.82,y2:y+s*.18,stroke:COL.T,'stroke-width':Math.max(1.6,s*.14),'stroke-linecap':'round'}));
      if(ties>1){var t=svgEl('text',{x:x+s/2,y:y+s*.66,'text-anchor':'middle','font-size':s*.5,'font-weight':900,fill:'#6ee7b7','font-family':'Inter,system-ui,sans-serif'});t.textContent=ties;G.s.appendChild(t);}}
    pairDots(G.s,x,y,s,c.h);
    if(i===BR.cells.length-1){G.s.appendChild(svgEl('circle',{cx:x+s/2,cy:y+s/2,r:s*.5,fill:'none',stroke:'rgba(242,210,126,.75)','stroke-width':1}));}
  });
  /* derived roads at half size */
  var hs=Math.max(5,Math.round(s/2));
  [['rEye',1,'eye'],['rSmall',2,'small'],['rRoach',3,'roach']].forEach(function(d){
    var marks=derivedMarks(BR.cols,d[1]).map(function(m){return {w:m};}),L=layoutRoad(marks),mc=0;L.cells.forEach(function(c){mc=Math.max(mc,c.c);});
    var g=gridSvg($(d[0]),hs,6,mc);
    L.cells.forEach(function(c){var x=(c.c-g.off)*hs,y=c.r*hs;if(x<0)return;var col=c.h.w==='R'?COL.R:COL.Bl;
      if(d[2]==='eye')g.s.appendChild(svgEl('circle',{cx:x+hs/2,cy:y+hs/2,r:hs*.32,fill:'none',stroke:col,'stroke-width':Math.max(1.2,hs*.2)}));
      else if(d[2]==='small')g.s.appendChild(svgEl('circle',{cx:x+hs/2,cy:y+hs/2,r:hs*.36,fill:col}));
      else g.s.appendChild(svgEl('line',{x1:x+hs*.22,y1:y+hs*.82,x2:x+hs*.78,y2:y+hs*.18,stroke:col,'stroke-width':Math.max(1.4,hs*.22),'stroke-linecap':'round'}));});
  });
  /* bead plate: one cell per coup, six down then the next column */
  var bead=$('rBead');
  if((!small||fsN)&&(!fsShort||$('roads').classList.contains('pop'))&&getComputedStyle(bead).display!=='none'){
    var rightH=$('rBig').offsetHeight+5+$('rEye').offsetHeight,bs=Math.max(12,Math.floor(rightH/6));
    var bg=gridSvg(bead,bs,6,Math.floor(Math.max(0,history.length-1)/6));
    history.forEach(function(h,i){var c=Math.floor(i/6),r=i%6,x=(c-bg.off)*bs,y=r*bs;if(x<0)return;
      bg.s.appendChild(svgEl('circle',{cx:x+bs/2,cy:y+bs/2,r:bs*.42,fill:COL[h.w]}));
      var t=svgEl('text',{x:x+bs/2,y:y+bs*.66,'text-anchor':'middle','font-size':bs*.46,'font-weight':900,fill:'#fff','font-family':'Inter,system-ui,sans-serif'});
      t.textContent=h.w==='T'?h.pt:(h.w==='P'?h.pt:h.bt);bg.s.appendChild(t);
      pairDots(bg.s,x,y,bs,h);});
  }
  /* header: shoe stats and the ask road */
  var n={P:0,B:0,T:0,pp:0,bp:0,nat:0};history.forEach(function(h){n[h.w]++;if(h.pp)n.pp++;if(h.bp)n.bp++;if(h.nat)n.nat++;});
  function ask(lbl,w){var a=askMarks(w),sv=a.map(function(m,i){if(!m)return '<svg viewBox="0 0 10 10"><circle cx="5" cy="5" r="3.4" fill="none" stroke="rgba(148,163,184,.35)" stroke-width="1.4"/></svg>';var c=m==='R'?COL.R:COL.Bl;
      return i===0?'<svg viewBox="0 0 10 10"><circle cx="5" cy="5" r="3.4" fill="none" stroke="'+c+'" stroke-width="2"/></svg>':i===1?'<svg viewBox="0 0 10 10"><circle cx="5" cy="5" r="4" fill="'+c+'"/></svg>':'<svg viewBox="0 0 10 10"><line x1="2" y1="8.5" x2="8" y2="1.5" stroke="'+c+'" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round"/></svg>';}).join('');
    return '<span title="What the derived roads would show if '+(w==='B'?'Banker':'Player')+' wins next" style="color:'+(w==='B'?'#fca5a5':'#93c5fd')+'">'+lbl+' '+sv+'</span>';}
  $('rhead').innerHTML='<span class="rt">Scoreboard</span><span class="rs"><i style="background:'+COL.B+'"></i>Banker <b>'+n.B+'</b></span><span class="rs"><i style="background:'+COL.P+'"></i>Player <b>'+n.P+'</b></span><span class="rs"><i style="background:'+COL.T+'"></i>Tie <b>'+n.T+'</b></span>'+
    '<span class="rs">Pairs <b>'+n.pp+'</b>&thinsp;/&thinsp;<b>'+n.bp+'</b></span><span class="rs">Naturals <b>'+n.nat+'</b></span><span class="rs">Coup <b>'+history.length+'</b></span>'+
    '<span class="rbrand" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="ask">'+ask('B?','B')+ask('P?','P')+'</span>';
}
function lastNonTie(){for(var i=history.length-1;i>=0;i--)if(history[i].w!=='T')return history[i].w;return null;}
function streakLen(){var w=lastNonTie(),n=0;for(var i=history.length-1;i>=0;i--){if(history[i].w==='T')continue;if(history[i].w===w)n++;else break;}return n;}

/* ---------- money + stats ---------- */
var bank=1000,start=1000,peak=1000,BOTSN=3,TALK=true;
var HAT='<svg class="hat" viewBox="0 0 30 14" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M1 11.5c3 1.6 25 1.6 28 0-2.3-.7-4.6-.9-6.3-1.3C22 6.6 21.4 1.4 18.4 1.4c-1.3 0-2 .9-3.4.9s-2.1-.9-3.4-.9c-3 0-3.6 5.2-4.3 8.8-1.7.4-4 .6-6.3 1.3z" fill="currentColor"/></svg>';
var ST={hands:0,w:0,l:0,pw:0,bw:0,t:0,nat:0,big:0,side:0,sq:0,comm:0};
var bets={},lastBets={},activeSides=['pp','bp','dbp'],target=null,chipV=25;

var MODE='solo';function liveClosed(){return MODE!=='solo'&&typeof LV!=='undefined'&&LV.closed();}
function betTotal(o){var t=0;for(var k in (o||bets))t+=(o||bets)[k]||0;return t;}

  $('bank').textContent=mf(bank);$('bank').title=money(bank);
  var net=bank+onT-start;$('net').textContent=(net>0?'+':'')+mf(net);$('net').title=money(net);$('net').style.color=net>0?'#34d399':net<0?'#fca5a5':'';
  $('handsN').textContent=ST.hands;$('winRate').textContent=(ST.w+ST.l)?Math.round(ST.w/(ST.w+ST.l)*100)+'%':'\u2013';
  $('myBank').textContent=cmoney(bank);
  peak=Math.max(peak,bank+onT);
  $('sW').textContent=ST.w;$('sL').textContent=ST.l;$('sPw').textContent=ST.pw;$('sBw').textContent=ST.bw;$('sT').textContent=ST.t;$('sNat').textContent=ST.nat;
  $('sBig').textContent=money(ST.big);$('sPeak').textContent=money(peak);$('sSide').textContent=(ST.side>0?'+':'')+money(ST.side);$('sShoes').textContent=shoesPlayed;

  if(document.documentElement.classList.contains('fs')&&typeof fitSoon==='function')fitSoon();
}
function msg(html,bad){var m=$('msg');m.innerHTML=html;m.classList.toggle('bad',!!bad);}
function toast(t){var el=$('toast');el.textContent=t;el.classList.add('show');clearTimeout(toast._t);toast._t=setTimeout(function(){el.classList.remove('show');},2800);}
var capT=null;
function caption(t,ms){var s=$('capS');$('capT').textContent=t;s.classList.add('on');unsched(capT);if(ms!==0)capT=sched(function(){s.classList.remove('on');},ms||2600);}
function chipClass(v){return v>=5000?'c5k':v>=1000?'c1k':v>=500?'c500':v>=100?'c100':v>=25?'c25':'c5';}
function chipsFor(a){var out=[],d=[5000,1000,500,100,25,5],x=Math.round(a);d.forEach(function(v){while(x>=v&&out.length<7){out.push(v);x-=v;}});if(!out.length&&a>0)out.push(5);return out;}
function stackHTML(a){var cs=chipsFor(a).reverse();return '<span class="cstack"><span class="pile" style="--n:'+cs.length+'">'+cs.map(function(v,i){return '<i class="'+chipClass(v)+'" style="bottom:'+(i*3)+'px"></i>';}).join('')+'</span><b>'+cmoney(a)+'</b></span>';}

/* ---------- betting spots ---------- */
function spotEl(id){return document.querySelector('#felt [data-spot="'+id+'"]');}
function renderSideSpots(){
  var box=$('sides'),have=[].map.call(box.querySelectorAll('.sspot'),function(x){return x.getAttribute('data-spot');});
  if(have.join()!==activeSides.join()){box.innerHTML='';
    activeSides.forEach(function(id){var d=sideDef(id);if(!d)return;var b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='sspot';b.setAttribute('data-spot',id);b.setAttribute('aria-describedby','btip');
      b.innerHTML='<b></b><small></small><span class="bdots"></span>';b.querySelector('small').textContent=d.short;box.appendChild(b);});}
}
function renderSpots(){
  renderSideSpots();
  [].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#felt [data-spot]'),function(el){
    var id=el.getAttribute('data-spot'),a=bets[id]||0,isZone=el.classList.contains('zone');
    el.classList.toggle('sel',id===target&&phase==='bet');el.classList.toggle('has',a>0);
    if(isZone){var z=el.querySelector('.zst'),key=a?'a'+a:'';if(z.getAttribute('data-k')!==key){z.setAttribute('data-k',key);z.innerHTML=a?stackHTML(a):'';}}
    else{el.querySelector('b').textContent=a?cmoney(a):'$0';}
    var nm=betName(id);el.setAttribute('aria-label',nm+' bet, '+money(a));
    /* other players' chips in their colors */
    var dots=el.querySelector('.bdots'),h='';
    seats.forEach(function(s){var v=s.bets&&s.bets[id];if(v)h+='<span class="bchip" title="'+esc(s.name)+' '+money(v)+'"><i style="background:'+s.col+'"></i><span>'+cmoney(v).replace('$','')+'</span></span>';});
    if(dots&&dots.getAttribute('data-h')!==h){dots.setAttribute('data-h',h);dots.innerHTML=h;}
  });
}
function renderSideMenu(){
  var box=$('sidelist');box.innerHTML='';
  SIDES.forEach(function(d){var on=activeSides.indexOf(d.id)>=0,ok=sideAllowed(d);
    var el=document.createElement('div');el.className='sbet'+(on?' on':'')+(ok?'':' off');
    el.innerHTML='<div class="top"><b></b><span style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center"><em></em><button class="tg" type="button"></button></span></div><details><summary>How it pays</summary><p></p><table></table></details>';
    el.querySelector('b').textContent=d.name;el.querySelector('em').textContent='House edge '+d.he;
    el.querySelector('p').textContent=d.about+(ok?'':' Only offered at an EZ Baccarat table.')+' House edge '+d.he+' (8 decks).';
    var tb=el.querySelector('table');d.table.forEach(function(r){var tr=document.createElement('tr');tr.innerHTML='<td></td><td></td>';tr.children[0].textContent=r[0];tr.children[1].textContent=fmtPay(r[1]);tb.appendChild(tr);});
    var tg=el.querySelector('.tg');tg.textContent=on?'On table':'Add';tg.disabled=!ok;
    tg.addEventListener('click',function(){
      if(phase!=='bet'||busy){toast('Change side bets between coups');return;}
      var i=activeSides.indexOf(d.id);
      if(i>=0){activeSides.splice(i,1);if(bets[d.id]){bank+=bets[d.id];delete bets[d.id];}if(target===d.id)target=null;}
      else{if(activeSides.length>=3){toast('A table has room for 3 side bets. Remove one first.');return;}activeSides.push(d.id);}
      renderSideMenu();hud();saveExtra();});
    box.appendChild(el);});
}
function enforceSides(){var ch=false;activeSides=activeSides.filter(function(id){var d=sideDef(id);var ok=d&&sideAllowed(d);if(!ok&&bets[id]){bank+=bets[id];delete bets[id];ch=true;}return ok;});
  seats.forEach(function(s){for(var k in s.bets)if(!MAIN[k]&&activeSides.indexOf(k)<0){s.bank+=s.bets[k];delete s.bets[k];}});return ch;}

/* ---------- hover tooltip on every bet ---------- */
var lastRes={};
var Tip=(function(){
  var el=null,timer=0,cur=null;
  function html(id){
    var a=bets[id]||0,rows,title,about,edge,lr=lastRes[id];
    if(id==='player'){title='Player';about='Bet that the Player hand finishes closer to 9. Pushes on a tie.';rows=[['Player wins','1 to 1'],['Tie','Push']];edge=EDGE.player.toFixed(2)+'%';}
    else if(id==='banker'){title='Banker';about='Bet that the Banker hand finishes closer to 9. Banker wins slightly more often, so it carries a commission or a special rule. Pushes on a tie.';
      rows=R.comm==='std'?[['Banker wins','0.95 to 1'],['Commission','5% of the win'],['Tie','Push']]:R.comm==='nc'?[['Banker wins','1 to 1'],['Banker wins with 6','1 to 2'],['Tie','Push']]:[['Banker wins','1 to 1'],['Banker wins with a 3-card 7','Push'],['Tie','Push']];edge=bankerEdge().toFixed(2)+'%';}
    else if(id==='tie'){title='Tie';about='Bet that both hands finish on the same total. About one coup in eleven is a tie.';rows=[['Tie',R.tie+' to 1']];edge=tieEdge().toFixed(2)+'%';}
    else{var d=sideDef(id);if(!d)return '';title=d.name;about=d.about;rows=d.table;edge=d.he;}
    var hit=lr&&lr.k==='win'?String(lr.label||'').toLowerCase():'';
    var res=lr?'<span class="rs'+(lr.k==='lose'?' l':'')+'">'+(lr.k==='win'?'Won '+esc(sgn(lr.profit))+(lr.label?' \u00b7 '+esc(lr.label):''):lr.k==='push'?'Pushed: bet returned':'Lost last coup')+'</span>':'';
    return '<div class="h"><b>'+esc(title)+'</b><span>'+(a?money(a):'No bet yet')+'</span></div>'+res+'<p>'+esc(about)+'</p><table>'+rows.map(function(r){var h=hit&&String(r[0]).toLowerCase()===hit;return '<tr'+(h?' class="hit"':'')+'><td>'+esc(r[0])+'</td><td>'+esc(fmtPay(r[1]))+'</td></tr>';}).join('')+'</table><small>House edge '+esc(edge)+' with 8 decks'+(phase==='bet'?' \u00b7 tap to drop a '+money(chipV)+' chip':'')+'</small>';
  }
  function place(anchor){
    var f=$('felt'),fr=f.getBoundingClientRect(),a=anchor.getBoundingClientRect(),w=el.offsetWidth,h=el.offsetHeight;
    var x=a.left-fr.left+a.width/2-w/2;x=Math.max(8,Math.min(fr.width-w-8,x));
    var y=a.top-fr.top-h-10;if(y<8)y=a.bottom-fr.top+10;
    el.style.left=x+'px';el.style.top=y+'px';
  }
  function show(anchor,id,ms){el=el||$('btip');clearTimeout(timer);var h=html(id);if(!h){hide();return;}cur=anchor;el.innerHTML=h;el.classList.add('on');place(anchor);if(ms)timer=setTimeout(hide,ms);}
  function hide(){if(!el)return;clearTimeout(timer);el.classList.remove('on');cur=null;}
  function idOf(t){var c=t.closest&&t.closest('[data-spot]');if(!c)return null;return {el:c,id:c.getAttribute('data-spot')};}
  var felt=$('felt'),lpT=0,lpFired=false;
  felt.addEventListener('pointerover',function(e){if(e.pointerType!=='mouse')return;var o=idOf(e.target);if(o)show(o.el,o.id);});
  felt.addEventListener('pointerout',function(e){if(e.pointerType!=='mouse')return;var o=idOf(e.target);if(o&&!(e.relatedTarget&&o.el.contains(e.relatedTarget)))hide();});
  /* touch: long-press a spot to read it (a quick tap still places a chip) */
  felt.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(e){if(e.pointerType==='mouse')return;var o=idOf(e.target);clearTimeout(lpT);lpFired=false;if(!o){hide();return;}lpT=setTimeout(function(){lpFired=true;show(o.el,o.id,4500);},450);});
  felt.addEventListener('pointerup',function(){clearTimeout(lpT);});felt.addEventListener('pointercancel',function(){clearTimeout(lpT);});
  felt.addEventListener('focusin',function(e){var o=idOf(e.target);if(o&&e.target.matches&&e.target.matches(':focus-visible'))show(o.el,o.id);});
  felt.addEventListener('focusout',function(){hide();});
  addEventListener('resize',function(){if(cur&&el&&el.classList.contains('on'))place(cur);});
  return {show:show,hide:hide,longPressed:function(){var f=lpFired;lpFired=false;return f;},refresh:function(){if(cur&&el&&el.classList.contains('on')){var o=idOf(cur);if(o&&document.contains(cur)){el.innerHTML=html(o.id);place(cur);}else hide();}}};
})();

/* ---------- flying chips ---------- */
function feltPt(el,dx,dy){var f=$('felt').getBoundingClientRect(),r=el.getBoundingClientRect();return {x:r.left-f.left+r.width/2+(dx||0),y:r.top-f.top+r.height/2+(dy||0)};}
function rackEl(){var r=$('rack');return r&&r.offsetWidth?r:$('crestFallback')||$('bt');}
function flyChip(from,to,cls,delay,dur,bg){
  var f=$('felt'),c=document.createElement('div');c.className='fchip '+(cls||'');if(bg)c.style.background=bg;f.appendChild(c);
  var a=from,b=to;c.style.transform='translate('+(b.x-12)+'px,'+(b.y-12)+'px)';c.style.opacity='0';
  var d=dur||(R.cine?520:300);
  if(c.animate){var an=c.animate([{transform:'translate('+(a.x-12)+'px,'+(a.y-12)+'px) scale(.9)',opacity:1},{transform:'translate('+((a.x+b.x)/2-12)+'px,'+(Math.min(a.y,b.y)-30-12)+'px) scale(1.1)',opacity:1,offset:.5},{transform:'translate('+(b.x-12)+'px,'+(b.y-12)+'px) scale(.95)',opacity:1}],{duration:d,delay:delay||0,easing:'cubic-bezier(.35,.1,.25,1)',fill:'both'});
    an.onfinish=function(){c.animate([{opacity:1},{opacity:0}],{duration:160,fill:'forwards'}).onfinish=function(){c.remove();};};}
  else c.remove();
  return later((delay||0)+d);
}

/* ---------- computer players: the regulars of the salon ---------- */

  human:{voice:'book',tag:'Player online',style:'a real person at this table'}
};

].map(function(a){return {id:'bot:'+a[0],key:a[0],name:a[1],col:a[2],sty:a[3]};});
var LINES={
  hello:{grinder:['Evening. Banker, as always.','Evening, all. Mind if I sit?'],streak:['Let me have a look at that road.','Evening. How is the shoe running?'],chop:['Evening. This shoe looks choppy to me.','Howdy, folks.'],

  broke:{grinder:['That is my limit for tonight. Good luck, all.'],streak:['The road ran out on me. Night, all.'],chop:['Out of chips. It never chopped back.'],tie:['I am tapped out! Somebody hit a tie for me.'],whale:['Busted! I will be back with more.']},
  cashout:{grinder:['Up nicely. Coloring up.'],streak:['Quitting while the road is good. Night.'],chop:['Taking my winnings home. Night, folks.'],tie:['Quitting while I am ahead, bye!'],whale:['Cashing out! The drinks are on me!']}
