var seats=[],benchSince={},roundNo=0,SLOTS=['L0','R0','L1','R1'];
function botsSeated(){return seats.length;}
function seatById(id){for(var i=0;i<seats.length;i++)if(seats[i].id===id)return seats[i];return null;}
function botList(){return seats.map(function(s){return {id:s.id,name:s.name,key:STY[s.sty].voice,col:s.col,tag:STY[s.sty].tag};});}
var CH=null;
function chatPost(s,txt){try{if(CH)CH.post(s.id,s.name,txt,s.col);}catch(e){}}
function sysChat(t){try{if(CH)CH.sys(t);}catch(e){}}
function say(s,txt,delay){
  if(!TALK||!s||!txt)return;
  sched(function(){if(seats.indexOf(s)<0&&!s.leaving)return;var b=s.el&&s.el.querySelector('.talk');if(b){b.textContent=txt;b.classList.add('on');unsched(s.talkT);s.talkT=sched(function(){b.classList.remove('on');},3400);}chatPost(s,txt);},delay||0);
}
function talk(s,ev,prob,delay,w){if(prob!==undefined&&Math.random()>prob)return;var p=LINES[ev];if(!p)return;var a=p[s.sty];if(!a)return;say(s,pick(a).replace('{w}',w==='P'?'Player':'Banker'),delay);}
function newSeat(d,slot){return {id:d.id,key:d.key,name:d.name,col:d.col,sty:d.sty,slot:slot,bank:d.sty==='whale'?5000:1000,start:d.sty==='whale'?5000:1000,bets:{},hands:0,lastNet:0,el:null,talkT:null,leaving:false};}
function nextRegular(){
  var taken={};seats.forEach(function(s){taken[s.id]=1;});
  var free=BOTDEF.filter(function(d){return !taken[d.id];});if(!free.length)return null;
  var fresh=free.filter(function(d){return !benchSince[d.id];});
  if(fresh.length)return pick(fresh);
  free.sort(function(a,b){return (benchSince[a.id]||0)-(benchSince[b.id]||0);});
  return pick(free.slice(0,Math.max(1,Math.ceil(free.length/2))));
}
function seatHTML(s){
  return '<div class="talk"></div><div class="sbets"></div><div class="plate"><div class="av" style="background:'+s.col+'">'+HAT+'<span>'+esc(s.name.charAt(0))+'</span></div><div class="pi"><div class="nm"></div><div class="ch"></div></div></div>';
}
function renderSeats(){
  var L=$('sideL'),Rr=$('sideR');
  var bySlot={};seats.forEach(function(s){bySlot[s.slot]=s;});
  function mount(box,order){var want=order.map(function(k){return bySlot[k];}).filter(Boolean);
    want.forEach(function(s){if(!s.el){s.el=document.createElement('div');s.el.className='seat enter';s.el.setAttribute('data-id',s.id);s.el.innerHTML=seatHTML(s);
      s.el.querySelector('.nm').textContent=s.name;s.el.title=s.name+' \u00b7 '+STY[s.sty].tag+': '+STY[s.sty].style;
      (function(el){sched(function(){el.classList.remove('enter');},40);})(s.el);}});
    var cur=[].slice.call(box.children).filter(function(c){return !c.classList.contains('leave');});
    var ok=cur.length===want.length&&cur.every(function(c,i){return c===want[i].el;});
    if(!ok){want.forEach(function(s){box.appendChild(s.el);});}
  }
  mount(L,['L1','L0']);mount(Rr,['R0','R1']);
  seats.forEach(function(s){updateSeat(s);});
  botWho();
}
function updateSeat(s){
  if(!s.el)return;if(s.human)s.el.querySelector('.nm').textContent=s.name;s.el.querySelector('.ch').textContent=cmoney(s.bank);
  var sb=s.el.querySelector('.sbets'),h='';
  for(var k in s.bets){var v=s.bets[k];if(!v)continue;var tg=k==='player'?'P':k==='banker'?'B':k==='tie'?'T':'S',lab=k==='player'?'P':k==='banker'?'B':k==='tie'?'Tie':(sideDef(k)||{short:k}).short;
    h+='<span class="bt2 '+tg+'"><i style="background:'+s.col+'"></i>'+esc(lab)+' '+cmoney(v)+'</span>';}
  if(s.res)h+=s.res;
  if(sb.getAttribute('data-h')!==h){sb.setAttribute('data-h',h);sb.innerHTML=h;if(document.documentElement.classList.contains('fs')&&typeof fitSoon==='function')fitSoon();}
}
function botWho(){
  var box=$('botWho');if(!box)return;
  box.innerHTML=seats.length?seats.map(function(s){return '<div><i style="background:'+s.col+'"></i><b>'+esc(s.name)+'</b> &middot; '+esc(STY[s.sty].tag)+'<br>'+esc(STY[s.sty].style)+'</div>';}).join(''):'<div>Just you and the dealer.</div>';
  try{if(CH)CH.refresh();}catch(e){}
}
function freeSlot(){var used={};seats.forEach(function(s){used[s.slot]=1;});for(var i=0;i<SLOTS.length;i++)if(!used[SLOTS[i]])return SLOTS[i];return null;}
function syncBots(initial){
  if(MODE!=='solo')return;
  /* add or remove seats to match the option (only between coups) */
  var arrived=[];
  while(seats.length>BOTSN){var s=seats.pop();if(s.el)s.el.remove();for(var k in s.bets)delete s.bets[k];}
  while(seats.length<BOTSN){var d=nextRegular();if(!d)break;var ns=newSeat(d,freeSlot());seats.push(ns);arrived.push(ns);}
  renderSeats();renderSpots();
  arrived.forEach(function(s,i){if(!initial)sysChat(s.name+' sat down');talk(s,'hello',initial?0.6:0.9,700+i*900);});
}
function chooseSide(s){
  var last=lastNonTie(),st=streakLen();
  if(s.sty==='grinder')return 'banker';
  if(s.sty==='streak')return last==='P'?'player':'banker';
  if(s.sty==='chop')return last==='B'?'player':last==='P'?'banker':'player';
  if(s.sty==='tie')return Math.random()<0.55?'banker':'player';
  /* whale: rides a streak of 2+, otherwise a coin flip leaning Banker */
  if(st>=2&&last)return last==='P'?'player':'banker';
  return Math.random()<0.6?'banker':'player';
}
function botBet(s){
  s.bets={};s.res='';var side=chooseSide(s),x=25,st=streakLen();
  if(s.sty==='grinder')x=s.key==='pearl'?10:s.key==='mabel'?15:25;
  else if(s.sty==='streak')x=25*Math.min(4,Math.max(1,st));
  else if(s.sty==='chop')x=pick([25,25,50]);
  else if(s.sty==='tie')x=pick([10,25,25]);
  else if(s.sty==='whale')x=s.lastNet<0?Math.min(Math.max(s.lastBet||200,200)*2,2000):pick([200,300,500,500,1000]);
  x=Math.max(5,Math.min(x,Math.floor(s.bank/5)*5));
  if(x<5)return;
  s.bets[side]=x;s.lastBet=x;var used=x;
  function side2(id,amt,p){if(activeSides.indexOf(id)>=0&&Math.random()<p&&s.bank>=used+amt){s.bets[id]=amt;used+=amt;}}
  if(s.sty==='tie'){var tb=history.length>=8&&!history.slice(-8).some(function(h){return h.w==='T';})?25:pick([5,10]);if(s.bank>=used+tb){s.bets.tie=tb;used+=tb;}side2('pp',5,.4);side2('bp',5,.4);side2('ep',5,.4);}
  if(s.sty==='whale'){side2(side==='player'?'dbp':'dbb',50,.4);side2('pp',25,.25);side2('bp',25,.25);side2('perf',25,.2);side2('dragon',25,.3);side2('panda',25,.3);}
  if(s.sty==='grinder')side2('bp',5,.12);
  if(s.sty==='streak')side2('big',25,.12);
  s.bank-=used;
  return side;
}
var botBetT=[];
function botsBet(cine){
  if(MODE!=='solo')return;
  botBetT.forEach(unsched);botBetT=[];
  seats.forEach(function(s,i){if(betTotal(s.bets)>0)return;
    botBetT.push(sched(function(){if(phase!=='bet'||seats.indexOf(s)<0||betTotal(s.bets)>0)return;var side=botBet(s);if(!side)return;
      var from=s.el?feltPt(s.el.querySelector('.av')):null;
      for(var k in s.bets){var sp=spotEl(k);if(from&&sp&&!document.documentElement.classList.contains('bpaused'))flyChip(from,feltPt(sp,0,6),'',0,R.cine?420:240,s.col);}
      SFX.chip();
      sched(function(){updateSeat(s);renderSpots();},R.cine?380:200);
      if(Math.random()<0.25||(s.sty==='streak'&&streakLen()>=3&&Math.random()<0.6))talk(s,'bet',1,500,side==='player'?'P':'B');},(cine?500:120)+i*(cine?650:150)+Math.random()*300));});
}
function botsBetNow(){if(MODE!=='solo')return;botBetT.forEach(unsched);botBetT=[];seats.forEach(function(s){if(betTotal(s.bets)===0){botBet(s);updateSeat(s);}});renderSpots();}
/* broke regulars leave, big winners sometimes cash out, and someone new sits down in that chair */
function botsChurn(){
  if(MODE!=='solo')return;
  var gone=[];
  seats.slice().forEach(function(s){
    var broke=s.bank<5,cash=!broke&&s.bank>=s.start*2.2&&s.hands>=8&&Math.random()<0.25;
    if(!broke&&!cash)return;
    s.leaving=true;talk(s,broke?'broke':'cashout',1,200);gone.push(s);benchSince[s.id]=Date.now();
  });
  gone.forEach(function(s,i){
    sched(function(){
      sysChat(s.name+' left the table');
      if(s.el){s.el.classList.add('leave');var el=s.el;sched(function(){el.remove();},450);}
      var ix=seats.indexOf(s);if(ix>=0)seats.splice(ix,1);
      sched(function(){if(seats.length>=BOTSN)return;var d=nextRegular();if(!d)return;var ns=newSeat(d,s.slot);seats.push(ns);renderSeats();sysChat(ns.name+' sat down');talk(ns,'hello',0.95,900);
        if(phase==='bet'&&!busy)botsBet(R.cine);},1600);
      renderSeats();renderSpots();
    },2600+i*500);
  });
}

/* ---------- the squeeze: peel a face-down card from any corner or edge ---------- */
var Squeeze=(function(){
  var ov,headEl,cardsEl,totEl,ctlEl,active=null;
  function clip(poly,f){/* keep the part of the polygon where f(p)>=0 */
    var out=[];for(var i=0;i<poly.length;i++){var a=poly[i],b=poly[(i+1)%poly.length],fa=f(a),fb=f(b);
      if(fa>=0)out.push(a);if((fa>=0)!==(fb>=0)){var t=fa/(fa-fb);out.push([a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*t,a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*t]);}}
    return out;}
  function area(p){var s=0;for(var i=0;i<p.length;i++){var a=p[i],b=p[(i+1)%p.length];s+=a[0]*b[1]-b[0]*a[1];}return Math.abs(s)/2;}
  function pts(p,ox,oy){return p.map(function(q){return (q[0]+(ox||0)).toFixed(1)+'px '+(q[1]+(oy||0)).toFixed(1)+'px';}).join(',');}
  function make(card,label){
    var el=document.createElement('div');el.className='sqc noidx';
    var front=makeCard(card,false);front.removeAttribute('data-id');el.appendChild(front);
    var cov=document.createElement('div');cov.className='cov';var back=makeCard(card,true);back.removeAttribute('data-id');back.removeAttribute('aria-label');cov.appendChild(back);el.appendChild(cov);
    var svg=svgEl('svg',{'aria-hidden':'true'});svg.style.cssText='position:absolute;left:-100%;top:-100%;width:300%;height:300%;overflow:visible;pointer-events:none';
    var uid='g'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8);
    svg.innerHTML='<defs><linearGradient id="'+uid+'f" gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#8b97a8"/><stop offset=".22" stop-color="#e9eef4"/><stop offset=".7" stop-color="#ffffff"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#dfe5ec"/></linearGradient>'+
      '<linearGradient id="'+uid+'s" gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#000" stop-opacity=".42"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#000" stop-opacity="0"/></linearGradient>'+
      '<filter id="'+uid+'b" x="-50%" y="-50%" width="200%" height="200%"><feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="5"/></filter></defs>'+
      '<polygon class="sh" fill="url(#'+uid+'s)"/><polygon class="ds" fill="#000" fill-opacity=".45" filter="url(#'+uid+'b)"/><polygon class="fp" fill="url(#'+uid+'f)" stroke="rgba(148,163,184,.6)" stroke-width="1"/>';
    el.appendChild(svg);
    var hint=document.createElement('div');hint.className='hint';el.appendChild(hint);
    var val=document.createElement('div');val.className='val';el.appendChild(val);
    var sq={el:el,card:card,cov:cov,svg:svg,uid:uid,val:val,C:null,P:null,frac:0,revealed:false,label:label,res:null};
    sq.done=new Promise(function(r){sq.res=r;});
    return sq;
  }
  function dims(sq){return {w:sq.el.offsetWidth,h:sq.el.offsetHeight};}
  function draw(sq){
    var D=dims(sq),w=D.w,h=D.h,C=sq.C,P=sq.P,svg=sq.svg;
    var g=svg.querySelector('linearGradient'),gs=svg.querySelectorAll('linearGradient')[1];
    svg.setAttribute('viewBox',(-w)+' '+(-h)+' '+(3*w)+' '+(3*h));
    if(!C||!P||Math.hypot(P[0]-C[0],P[1]-C[1])<1.5){sq.cov.style.clipPath='';['sh','ds','fp'].forEach(function(c){svg.querySelector('.'+c).setAttribute('points','');});sq.frac=0;return 0;}
    var dx=P[0]-C[0],dy=P[1]-C[1],d=Math.hypot(dx,dy),nx=dx/d,ny=dy/d,mx=(C[0]+P[0])/2,my=(C[1]+P[1])/2;
    function f(q){return (q[0]-mx)*nx+(q[1]-my)*ny;}
    var rect=[[0,0],[w,0],[w,h],[0,h]];
    var keep=clip(rect,f),fold=clip(rect,function(q){return -f(q);});
    var refl=fold.map(function(q){var v=f(q);return [q[0]-2*v*nx,q[1]-2*v*ny];});
    sq.cov.style.clipPath=keep.length>2?'polygon('+pts(keep)+')':'polygon(0 0,0 0,0 0)';
    var P2=function(p){return p.map(function(q){return q[0].toFixed(1)+','+q[1].toFixed(1);}).join(' ');};
    svg.querySelector('.fp').setAttribute('points',P2(refl));
    svg.querySelector('.ds').setAttribute('points',P2(refl.map(function(q){return [q[0]-nx*6+3,q[1]-ny*6+5];})));
    /* shading: dark at the fold line, light toward the lifted tip */
    var tip=refl.reduce(function(m,q){var v=(q[0]-mx)*nx+(q[1]-my)*ny;return v>m.v?{v:v,q:q}:m;},{v:0,q:[mx,my]});
    g.setAttribute('x1',mx);g.setAttribute('y1',my);g.setAttribute('x2',mx+nx*Math.max(8,tip.v));g.setAttribute('y2',my+ny*Math.max(8,tip.v));
    /* the revealed face sits in the shadow of the lifted flap near the fold */
    svg.querySelector('.sh').setAttribute('points',P2(fold));
    gs.setAttribute('x1',mx);gs.setAttribute('y1',my);gs.setAttribute('x2',mx-nx*Math.min(w,h)*.22);gs.setAttribute('y2',my-ny*Math.min(w,h)*.22);
    sq.frac=fold.length>2?area(fold)/(w*h):0;
    return sq.frac;
  }
  function startFrom(sq,x,y){
    var D=dims(sq),w=D.w,h=D.h,k=Math.min(w,h)*0.36,corners=[[0,0],[w,0],[w,h],[0,h]],best=null;
    corners.forEach(function(c){var dd=Math.hypot(x-c[0],y-c[1]);if(dd<k&&(!best||dd<best.d))best={d:dd,c:c};});
    if(best)return best.c.slice();
    var ed=[[x,0,y],[x,h,h-y],[0,y,x],[w,y,w-x]].sort(function(a,b){return a[2]-b[2];})[0];
    return [Math.max(0,Math.min(w,ed[0])),Math.max(0,Math.min(h,ed[1]))];
  }
  function finish(sq,fast){
    if(sq.revealed)return Promise.resolve();sq.revealed=true;
    var D=dims(sq),C=sq.C||[D.w/2,D.h],P0=sq.P||C,far=[D.w-C[0]+ (C[0]-D.w/2)*0.2,D.h-C[1]];
    /* throw the cover over: fold line sweeps past the far edge */
    var tgt=[C[0]+(far[0]-C[0])*2.3,C[1]+(far[1]-C[1])*2.3];
    if(Math.hypot(tgt[0]-C[0],tgt[1]-C[1])<4)tgt=[C[0],C[1]-D.h*2.3];
    sq.C=C;SFX.flip();
    return tween(fast?120:260,function(k){var e=1-Math.pow(1-k,3);sq.P=[P0[0]+(tgt[0]-P0[0])*e,P0[1]+(tgt[1]-P0[1])*e];draw(sq);}).then(function(){
      sq.cov.style.display='none';sq.svg.style.display='none';sq.el.classList.remove('noidx');sq.el.classList.add('done');
      sq.val.textContent=RANK_TXT[sq.card.r]+' \u00b7 counts '+bv(sq.card);SFX.card();updTot();sq.res();});
  }
  function springBack(sq){var C=sq.C,P0=sq.P;if(!C||!P0)return;return tween(240,function(k){var e=k*k;sq.P=[P0[0]+(C[0]-P0[0])*e,P0[1]+(C[1]-P0[1])*e];draw(sq);});}
  function bindHuman(sq){
    var drag=false,lastSnd=0;
    function loc(e){var r=sq.el.getBoundingClientRect();return [e.clientX-r.left,e.clientY-r.top];}
    sq.el.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(e){if(sq.revealed||sq.auto)return;e.preventDefault();var p=loc(e);sq.C=startFrom(sq,p[0],p[1]);sq.P=p;drag=true;sq.el.classList.add('grab','touched');
      try{sq.el.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_){}draw(sq);SFX.peel();});
    sq.el.addEventListener('pointermove',function(e){if(!drag||sq.revealed)return;sq.P=loc(e);var fr=draw(sq);var now=Date.now();if(now-lastSnd>110){lastSnd=now;SFX.peel();}
      if(fr>0.66){drag=false;sq.el.classList.remove('grab');finish(sq);}});
    function up(){if(!drag)return;drag=false;sq.el.classList.remove('grab');if(sq.revealed)return;if(sq.frac>0.48)finish(sq);else springBack(sq);}
    sq.el.addEventListener('pointerup',up);sq.el.addEventListener('pointercancel',up);
  }
  /* an automatic squeeze: short side first, a pause, then the long side, then the flip */
  async function autoSq(sq,style){
    if(sq.revealed)return;sq.auto=true;sq.el.classList.add('touched');
    var D=dims(sq),w=D.w,h=D.h,slow=style==='whale'?1.15:style==='quick'?0.5:0.85;
    async function peelTo(C,P,ms){sq.C=C;var P0=sq.P&&sq.C===C?sq.P:C.slice();await tween(ms*slow,function(k){if(sq.revealed)return;var e=k<.5?2*k*k:1-Math.pow(-2*k+2,2)/2;sq.P=[P0[0]+(P[0]-P0[0])*e,P0[1]+(P[1]-P0[1])*e];draw(sq);});}
    var Cb=[w*0.62,h];
    await peelTo(Cb,[w*0.5,h-h*0.34],700);if(sq.revealed)return;SFX.beat();
    await later(420*slow);if(sq.revealed)return;
    await peelTo(Cb,[w*0.46,h-h*0.6],620);if(sq.revealed)return;SFX.peel();
    await later(380*slow);if(sq.revealed)return;
    await springBack(sq);if(sq.revealed)return;
    if(style==='whale'){SFX.breath();puff();await later(500);}
    var Cs=[w,h*0.42];sq.P=null;
    await peelTo(Cs,[w-w*0.62,h*0.48],720);if(sq.revealed)return;SFX.beat();
    await later(480*slow);if(sq.revealed)return;
    await finish(sq);
  }
  function puff(){var p=document.createElement('div');p.className='puff on';cardsEl.appendChild(p);sched(function(){p.remove();},950);}
  function updTot(){if(!active)return;var shown=active.sqs.filter(function(s){return s.revealed;}).map(function(s){return s.card;});
    var base=active.base||[];var all=base.concat(shown);
    totEl.innerHTML=shown.length?(active.sideName+' <b>'+tot(all)+'</b>'+(active.sqs.length>shown.length?' so far':'')):'';}
  function open(o){
    ov=$('sqz');headEl=$('sqHead');cardsEl=$('sqCards');totEl=$('sqTot');ctlEl=$('sqCtl');
    var who=o.who,sideName=o.side==='P'?'Player':'Banker',isMe=who.kind==='me';
    headEl.innerHTML='<div class="av" style="background:'+(isMe?'#10b981':who.seat.col)+'">'+HAT+'<span>'+(isMe?'Y':esc(who.seat.name.charAt(0)))+'</span></div><div><b>'+(isMe?'Your squeeze':esc(who.seat.name)+' squeezes')+'<span class="side '+o.side+'">'+sideName+'</span></b><span>'+
      (isMe?(o.third?'Third card. Drag a corner or an edge to peel it.':'Biggest '+sideName+' bet squeezes. Drag a corner or an edge to peel each card.'):(o.third?'Third card for the '+sideName+'.':'Biggest '+sideName+' bet at the table gets to squeeze.'))+'</span></div>';
    cardsEl.innerHTML='';ctlEl.innerHTML='';totEl.innerHTML='';
    var sqs=o.cards.map(function(c){return make(c);});
    sqs.forEach(function(s){cardsEl.appendChild(s.el);});
    active={sqs:sqs,sideName:sideName,base:o.base||[]};
    ov.classList.add('on');
    return sqs;
  }
  function close(){if(ov)ov.classList.remove('on');active=null;document.removeEventListener('keydown',keyH,true);}
  var keyH=null;
  async function run(o){
    var sqs=open(o),isMe=o.who.kind==='me';
    if(o.base&&o.base.length)updTot();
    if(isMe){
      ST.sq++;
      sqs.forEach(bindHuman);
      var au=document.createElement('button');au.className='btn2';au.type='button';au.textContent='Auto-squeeze';
      var tv=document.createElement('button');tv.className='btn';tv.type='button';tv.textContent='Turn '+(sqs.length>1?'them':'it')+' over';
      ctlEl.appendChild(au);ctlEl.appendChild(tv);
      var autoRun=false;
      async function doAuto(){if(autoRun)return;autoRun=true;au.disabled=true;for(var i=0;i<sqs.length;i++){if(!sqs[i].revealed)await autoSq(sqs[i],i>0?'quick':'');}}
      au.addEventListener('click',doAuto);
      tv.addEventListener('click',function(){au.disabled=true;tv.disabled=true;sqs.forEach(function(s,i){sched(function(){finish(s,true);},i*160);});});
      keyH=function(e){if(e.key===' '||e.key==='Spacebar'){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();doAuto();}else if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();tv.click();}};
      document.addEventListener('keydown',keyH,true);
      if(window.__baccarat_autoSqueeze)sched(doAuto,200);
      /* the table is waiting: after a while without a touch, the dealer squeezes for you */
      var idleT=sched(function(){if(!sqs.some(function(s){return s.el.classList.contains('touched');})){toast('The table is waiting, so the dealer squeezes for you');doAuto();}},30000);
      Promise.all(sqs.map(function(s){return s.done;})).then(function(){unsched(idleT);});
    } else {
      var sk=document.createElement('button');sk.className='btn2';sk.type='button';sk.textContent='Skip';ctlEl.appendChild(sk);
      sk.addEventListener('click',function(){sk.disabled=true;sqs.forEach(function(s){finish(s,true);});});
      (async function(){await later(350);for(var i=0;i<sqs.length;i++){if(!sqs[i].revealed){if(i===0||Math.random()<0.6)talk(o.who.seat,'squeeze',0.85,0);await autoSq(sqs[i],i>0?'quick':(o.who.seat.sty==='whale'?'whale':''));}}})();
    }
    await Promise.all(sqs.map(function(s){return s.done;}));
    if(ctlEl)ctlEl.innerHTML='';
    await later(R.cine?850:300);
    close();
    await later(260);
  }
  return {run:run,active:function(){return !!active;},skipAll:function(){if(active)active.sqs.forEach(function(s){finish(s,true);});}};
})();

/* ---------- dealing ---------- */
var coupNo=0,cutCoup=-1;
function slotOf(side,i){return $('s'+side+(i+1));}
function placeCard(side,i,c,down){
  var slot=slotOf(side,i),el=makeCard(c,down);slot.innerHTML='';slot.appendChild(el);
  var third=i===2&&!document.documentElement.classList.contains('fsnarrow'),end=third?'rotate(90deg)':'none';
  try{var mo=$('mouth');if(mo.animate&&mo.offsetWidth)mo.animate([{transform:'skewX(-18deg)'},{transform:'skewX(-18deg) translateX(-7px)'},{transform:'skewX(-18deg)'}],{duration:220,easing:'ease-out'});}catch(e){}
  try{var m=$('mouth').getBoundingClientRect(),r=el.getBoundingClientRect();
    if(!m.width){var bt=$('bt').getBoundingClientRect();m={left:bt.right-40,top:bt.top+10,width:10,height:10};}
    var dx=m.left+m.width/2-(r.left+r.width/2),dy=m.top+m.height/2-(r.top+r.height/2);
    if(el.animate)el.animate([{transform:'translate('+dx+'px,'+dy+'px) rotate(-24deg) scale(.6)',opacity:1},{transform:'translate('+(dx*0.12)+'px,'+(dy*0.12)+'px) '+(third?'rotate(80deg)':'rotate(-3deg)')+' scale(1.02)',opacity:1,offset:.78},{transform:end,opacity:1}],{duration:R.cine?560:260,easing:'cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.3,1)'});
  }catch(e){}
  R.cine?SFX.slide():SFX.card();
  return el;
}
function flipUp(side,i){var el=slotOf(side,i).querySelector('.card');if(el&&el.classList.contains('down')){el.classList.remove('down');el.setAttribute('aria-label',RANK_TXT[(side==='P'?hand.p:hand.b)[i].r]+' of '+SUIT_NAME[(side==='P'?hand.p:hand.b)[i].s]);}}
function showTot(side,cards){var t=$(side==='P'?'totP':'totB');t.textContent=cards.length?String(tot(cards)):'';t.classList.remove('bump');void t.offsetWidth;if(cards.length)t.classList.add('bump');}
function squeezerFor(side){
  var spot=side==='P'?'player':'banker',mine=bets[spot]||0,best=null,bv2=0;
  if(MODE!=='solo')return mine>0?{kind:'me'}:null;
  seats.forEach(function(s){var v=s.bets[spot]||0;if(v>bv2){bv2=v;best=s;}});
  if(mine>0&&mine>=bv2)return {kind:'me'};
  if(best)return {kind:'bot',seat:best};
  return null;
}
function setWho(side,who){var el=$(side==='P'?'whoP':'whoB');if(!who){el.classList.remove('on');return;}el.textContent=who.kind==='me'?'Your squeeze':who.seat.name+' squeezes';el.classList.add('on');}
function markSqueezer(who,on){if(who&&who.kind==='bot'&&who.seat.el)who.seat.el.classList.toggle('sq',on);if(who&&who.kind==='me')$('mySeat').classList.toggle('sq',on);}
async function reveal(side,idxs,third){
  var cards=side==='P'?hand.p:hand.b,name=side==='P'?'Player':'Banker';
  if(!R.cine){idxs.forEach(function(i){flipUp(side,i);});showTot(side,cards);return;}
  var who=R.squeeze?squeezerFor(side):null;
  if(who){
    markSqueezer(who,true);
    caption(who.kind==='me'?'Your squeeze, '+name+' '+(third?'third card':'cards'):(who.seat.name+' squeezes the '+name+' '+(third?'third card':'cards')),0);
    await Squeeze.run({cards:idxs.map(function(i){return cards[i];}),side:side,who:who,third:third,base:third?cards.slice(0,2):[]});
    markSqueezer(who,false);
    idxs.forEach(function(i){flipUp(side,i);});SFX.flip();
  } else {
    caption(third?'Dealer turns the '+name+' third card':'Dealer turns the '+name+' cards',0);
    await later(420);
    for(var k=0;k<idxs.length;k++){flipUp(side,idxs[k]);SFX.flip();await later(380);}
  }
  showTot(side,cards);
}
function announce(big,sub,cls){var a=$('announce');$('annBig').textContent=big;$('annSub').textContent=sub||'';$('annSub').style.display=sub?'':'none';a.className='announce '+(cls||'');void a.offsetWidth;a.classList.add('on');
  var fl=$('flash');fl.classList.remove('on');void fl.offsetWidth;fl.classList.add('on');}
async function sweepTable(){
  var els=[].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('#bt .slot .card'));
  $('boxP').classList.remove('win','lost','tie');$('boxB').classList.remove('win','lost','tie');
  document.querySelectorAll('#felt .won').forEach(function(x){x.classList.remove('won');});
  if(els.length){
    var tray=$('trayBin').getBoundingClientRect();if(!tray.width){var b=$('bt').getBoundingClientRect();tray={left:b.left+20,top:b.top+10,width:10,height:10};}
    els.forEach(function(el,i){var r=el.getBoundingClientRect(),dx=tray.left+tray.width/2-(r.left+r.width/2),dy=tray.top+tray.height/2-(r.top+r.height/2);
      if(el.animate)el.animate([{transform:getComputedStyle(el).transform==='none'?'none':getComputedStyle(el).transform},{transform:'translate('+dx+'px,'+dy+'px) rotate(8deg) scale(.4)',opacity:.15}],{duration:R.cine?340:200,delay:i*30,easing:'ease-in',fill:'forwards'});});
    SFX.card();
    await later((R.cine?360:220)+els.length*30);
    discards+=els.length;
  }
  ['P','B'].forEach(function(s){for(var i=0;i<3;i++)slotOf(s,i).innerHTML='';});
  $('totP').textContent='';$('totB').textContent='';hand={p:[],b:[]};shoeHud();
  setWho('P',null);setWho('B',null);
}

/* ---------- a coup from "No more bets" to the payout ---------- */
async function deal(liveCards){
  if(phase!=='bet'||busy)return;
  var live=!!liveCards;
  if(!live&&MODE!=='solo'){if(betTotal()<5){toast('Place a bet first');return;}LV.wantDeal();return;}
  if(!live&&betTotal()<5){toast(bank<5?'Rebuy to keep playing':'Place a bet first: tap Player, Banker or Tie');return;}
  busy=true;phase='deal';Tip.hide();lastRes={};
  if(betTotal()>0){lastBets=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(bets));saveExtra();}
  setButtons();renderSpots();
  await sweepTable();
  seats.forEach(function(s){s.res='';updateSeat(s);});
  if(!live&&needShuffle){
    var first=shoesPlayed===1&&!totalCards;
    if(R.shufCine){await Ceremony.run(first);}
    else{msg('Shuffling a fresh eight-deck shoe\u2026');$('felt').classList.add('shuffling');SFX.shuffle();await later(1100);$('felt').classList.remove('shuffling');var b=buildShoeQuick();toast('New shoe \u00b7 burn card '+RANK_TXT[b.card.r]+', '+b.n+' more burned');}
    if(!first)shoesPlayed++;
    needShuffle=false;history=[];cutCoup=-1;drawRoads();
  }
  botsBetNow();
  coupNo++;
  var cine=R.cine;
  msg(cine?'<b>No more bets.</b>':'Dealing\u2026');
  caption('No more bets',cine?1600:900);
  await later(cine?750:120);
  var order=[['P',0],['B',0],['P',1],['B',1]];
  for(var k=0;k<4;k++){var o=order[k],c=drawCard();if(cutReached&&cutCoup<0)cutCoup=coupNo;(o[0]==='P'?hand.p:hand.b).push(c);placeCard(o[0],o[1],c,cine);await later(cine?430:140);}
  if(cine){var wp=R.squeeze?squeezerFor('P'):null,wb=R.squeeze?squeezerFor('B'):null;setWho('P',wp);setWho('B',wb);}
  else{showTot('P',hand.p);showTot('B',hand.b);}
  await later(cine?300:80);
  msg(cine?'Revealing the cards\u2026':'Dealing\u2026');
  await reveal('P',[0,1],false);
  var p2=tot(hand.p);caption('Player shows '+p2+(p2>=8?', a natural':''),cine?2200:1200);
  await later(cine?650:150);
  await reveal('B',[0,1],false);
  var b2=tot(hand.b);caption('Banker shows '+b2+(b2>=8?', a natural':''),cine?2200:1200);
  await later(cine?750:180);
  var natural=p2>=8||b2>=8;
  if(natural){
    var nb=Math.max(p2,b2),who2=p2>=8&&b2>=8?'Both hands':p2>b2?'Player':b2>p2?'Banker':'Both hands';
    if(p2>=8&&b2>=8&&p2!==b2)who2=p2>b2?'Player':'Banker';
    caption('Natural '+nb+(p2>=8&&b2>=8?' against a natural '+Math.min(p2,b2):' for the '+who2)+'. No more cards.',2600);
    if(cine){announce('Natural '+nb+'!',who2==='Both hands'?'Both hands':who2,who2==='Player'?'p':who2==='Banker'?'b':'');SFX.natural();await later(1500);}
    ST.nat++;
  } else {
    var p3=null;
    if(p2<=5){
      caption('Player has '+p2+' and draws',cine?1800:900);await later(cine?700:150);
      var c3=drawCard();if(cutReached&&cutCoup<0)cutCoup=coupNo;hand.p.push(c3);placeCard('P',2,c3,cine);await later(cine?520:160);
      await reveal('P',[2],true);
      p3=bv(c3);caption('Player draws '+(c3.r===1||c3.r===8?'an ':'a ')+RANK_TXT[c3.r]+'. Player '+tot(hand.p),cine?2200:1200);await later(cine?800:180);
    } else {caption('Player stands on '+p2,cine?1800:1000);await later(cine?800:180);}
    var bd=bankerDraws(b2,p3),why=p3===null?'':' against a third card '+p3;
    if(bd){
      caption('Banker has '+b2+' and draws'+why,cine?2000:1000);await later(cine?800:160);
      var c4=drawCard();if(cutReached&&cutCoup<0)cutCoup=coupNo;hand.b.push(c4);placeCard('B',2,c4,cine);await later(cine?520:160);
      await reveal('B',[2],true);
      caption('Banker draws '+(c4.r===1||c4.r===8?'an ':'a ')+RANK_TXT[c4.r]+'. Banker '+tot(hand.b),cine?2200:1200);await later(cine?800:180);
    } else {caption('Banker stands on '+b2+why,cine?2000:1000);await later(cine?850:180);}
  }
  showTot('P',hand.p);showTot('B',hand.b);setWho('P',null);setWho('B',null);
  await resolve();
}
async function resolve(){
  var x=coupCtx(hand.p,hand.b),cine=R.cine;
  var rt=x.w==='T'?'Tie, '+x.pt+' to '+x.bt:(x.w==='P'?'Player':'Banker')+' wins '+(x.w==='P'?x.pt:x.bt)+' to '+(x.w==='P'?x.bt:x.pt);
  if(x.w==='P'){$('boxP').classList.add('win');$('boxB').classList.add('lost');}
  else if(x.w==='B'){$('boxB').classList.add('win');$('boxP').classList.add('lost');}
  else{$('boxP').classList.add('tie');$('boxB').classList.add('tie');}
  var dragon7=R.comm==='ez'&&x.w==='B'&&x.b.length===3&&x.bt===7,panda8=x.w==='P'&&x.p.length===3&&x.pt===8;
  caption(rt+(dragon7?'. Dragon 7!':panda8&&R.comm==='ez'?'. Panda 8!':''),3200);
  if(cine){announce(x.w==='T'?'Tie':(x.w==='P'?'Player':'Banker')+' wins',(x.w==='T'?x.pt+' all':(x.w==='P'?x.pt+' to '+x.bt:x.bt+' to '+x.pt))+(dragon7?' \u00b7 Dragon 7':''),x.w==='P'?'p':x.w==='B'?'b':'t');}
  x.w==='T'?SFX.tie():null;
  var zone=spotEl(x.w==='P'?'player':x.w==='B'?'banker':'tie');if(zone)zone.classList.add('won');
  history.push({w:x.w,pt:x.pt,bt:x.bt,pp:x.pp,bp:x.bp,nat:x.nat});drawRoads();
  if(x.w==='P')ST.pw++;else if(x.w==='B')ST.bw++;else ST.t++;
  await later(cine?900:200);
  /* settle every bet on the layout: the dealer takes the losers, then pays the winners */
  var parts=[],net=0,pays=[],rack=rackEl(),rp=feltPt(rack),myP=feltPt($('myAv')),d=0,dd=cine?70:30;
  Object.keys(bets).forEach(function(id){var a=bets[id];if(!a)return;var r=settleBet(id,a,x);lastRes[id]=r;net+=r.profit;
    parts.push(betName(id)+' '+(r.k==='push'?'push':sgn(r.profit)));
    if(r.comm)ST.comm+=r.comm;if(!MAIN[id])ST.side+=r.profit;
    var sp=spotEl(id);if(sp&&r.k==='win')sp.classList.add('won');
    pays.push({who:null,id:id,a:a,r:r,sp:sp});});
  seats.forEach(function(s){var sn=0;Object.keys(s.bets).forEach(function(id){var a=s.bets[id];if(!a)return;var r=settleBet(id,a,x);sn+=r.profit;pays.push({who:s,id:id,a:a,r:r,sp:spotEl(id)});});s.lastNet=sn;s.hands++;
    s.res=sn>0?'<span class="res w">'+sgn(sn)+'</span>':sn<0?'<span class="res l">'+sgn(sn)+'</span>':'<span class="res e">Even</span>';});
  /* 1. losers swept to the dealer */
  var lose=pays.filter(function(p){return p.r.k==='lose';}),win=pays.filter(function(p){return p.r.k!=='lose';}),anims=[];
  lose.forEach(function(p,i){if(p.sp)anims.push(flyChip(feltPt(p.sp,0,4),rp,p.who?'':chipClass(p.a),i*dd,cine?480:260,p.who?p.who.col:''));
    (function(p){sched(function(){if(p.who)delete p.who.bets[p.id];else delete bets[p.id];renderSpots();seats.forEach(updateSeat);},i*dd+60);})(p);});
  if(lose.length)SFX.chips(Math.min(8,lose.length+2));
  await Promise.all(anims);
  if(lose.length&&win.length)await later(cine?250:80);
  /* 2. winners paid from the rack, then everything goes back to its owner */
  anims=[];
  win.forEach(function(p,i){var dl=i*dd,dst=p.who?(p.who.el?feltPt(p.who.el.querySelector('.av')):rp):myP;
    var pr=Promise.resolve();
    if(p.r.k==='win'&&p.sp)pr=flyChip(rp,feltPt(p.sp,0,4),p.who?'':chipClass(p.r.profit),dl,cine?480:260,p.who?p.who.col:'');
    anims.push(pr.then(function(){return later(cine?260:60);}).then(function(){if(p.who)delete p.who.bets[p.id];else delete bets[p.id];renderSpots();seats.forEach(updateSeat);
      return p.sp?flyChip(feltPt(p.sp,0,4),dst,p.who?'':chipClass(p.a+p.r.profit),0,cine?460:240,p.who?p.who.col:''):null;}).then(function(){
      if(p.who){p.who.bank+=p.a+p.r.profit;updateSeat(p.who);}else{bank+=p.a+p.r.profit;hud();}}));});
  if(win.length)sched(function(){SFX.chips(Math.min(8,win.length+3));},cine?200:60);
  await Promise.all(anims);
  bets={};seats.forEach(function(s){s.bets={};updateSeat(s);});renderSpots();
  /* the hand's result, one net line */
  ST.hands++;if(net>0)ST.w++;else if(net<0)ST.l++;if(net>ST.big)ST.big=net;
  var lead=rt+'.',brk='<small>('+parts.map(esc).join(' \u00b7 ')+')</small>';
  if(net>0){msg(lead+' You win <b>'+money(net)+'</b>'+(parts.length>1?' net':'')+brk);x.nat&&cine?SFX.natural():SFX.win();setTimeout(function(){suBanner(x.w==='T'?'Tie Pays!':x.nat?'Natural!':'Winner!','+'+money(net));},cine?700:0);}
  else if(net<0){msg(lead+' You lose <b>'+money(-net)+'</b>'+(parts.length>1?' net':'')+brk,true);SFX.lose();}
  else msg(lead+' <b>Even</b> on the coup.'+brk);
  if(net>0)$('mySeat').classList.add('won');sched(function(){$('mySeat').classList.remove('won');},2200);
  /* the regulars react */
  var talkers=seats.filter(function(s){return s.lastNet!==0||x.w==='T';});
  talkers.sort(function(){return Math.random()-.5;}).slice(0,2).forEach(function(s,i){
    var ev=x.w==='T'&&(s.sty==='tie'||Math.random()<.4)?'tiewin':x.nat&&Math.random()<.4?'natural':s.lastNet>0?'win':'lose';
    talk(s,ev,s.sty==='whale'?0.85:0.55,600+i*1300);
    if(s.el&&s.lastNet>0){s.el.classList.add('won');(function(el){sched(function(){el.classList.remove('won');},2200);})(s.el);}});
  seats.forEach(updateSeat);
  /* end of the shoe? one more coup after the cut card, then shuffle */
  if(cutReached){if(coupNo>cutCoup){needShuffle=true;sched(function(){toast('Last coup of the shoe is done. Fresh shoe on the next deal.');},1200);}else sched(function(){toast('Cut card is out: one more coup, then a fresh shoe');},1200);}
  if(pendingR){var pr2=pendingR;pendingR=null;for(var k in pr2)R[k]=pr2[k];applyRuleUI();}
  phase='bet';busy=false;target=target||null;
  /* the regulars place their next bets; broke ones leave */
  if(MODE==='solo'){botsChurn();if(seats.length<BOTSN)syncBots(false);botsBet(R.cine);}
  else{if(MODE==='view')needShuffle=false;LV.done();}
  hud();
}

/* ---------- controls ---------- */
function setButtons(){
  var bet=phase==='bet'&&!busy&&!liveClosed(),t=betTotal();
  $('dealBtn').innerHTML=MODE==='solo'?'Deal &rarr;':(typeof LV!=='undefined'&&LV.wanted()?'Ready &#10003;':'Ready &rarr;');
  $('dealBtn').disabled=!bet||t<5;
  $('clearBtn').disabled=!bet||t===0;
  var lb=betTotal(lastBets);$('rebetBtn').disabled=!bet||lb===0||(t===0&&bank<lb);
  $('dblBtn').disabled=!bet||(t===0?bank<lb*2||lb===0:bank<t);
  [].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#chips .chip'),function(c){c.disabled=!bet||bank<+c.getAttribute('data-v');c.classList.toggle('on',+c.getAttribute('data-v')===chipV);});
  var broke=bet&&bank<5&&t===0;$('rebuyBtn').classList.toggle('hide',!broke);$('dealBtn').classList.toggle('hide',broke);
}
function addBet(id,v,fromEl){
  if(phase!=='bet'||busy||liveClosed())return false;
  if(id!=='player'&&id!=='banker'&&id!=='tie'&&activeSides.indexOf(id)<0)return false;
  if(bank<v){toast('Not enough chips for that');return false;}
  bank-=v;bets[id]=(bets[id]||0)+v;
  var sp=spotEl(id);if(sp&&fromEl)flyChip(feltPt(fromEl),feltPt(sp,0,4),chipClass(v),0,R.cine?320:200);
  SFX.chip();hud();return true;
}
function clearBets(force){if(!force&&(phase!=='bet'||busy||liveClosed()))return;bank+=betTotal();bets={};lastRes={};hud();}
function rebet(){if(phase!=='bet'||busy||liveClosed())return;var lb=lastBets;if(!betTotal(lb))return;clearBets();if(bank<betTotal(lb)){toast('Not enough chips to rebet');return;}
  for(var k in lb){if(MAIN[k]||activeSides.indexOf(k)>=0){bank-=lb[k];bets[k]=lb[k];}}SFX.chips(4);hud();}
function doubleBets(){if(phase!=='bet'||busy||liveClosed())return;if(!betTotal()){rebet();}var t=betTotal();if(!t)return;if(bank<t){toast('Not enough chips to double');return;}for(var k in bets){bank-=bets[k];bets[k]*=2;}SFX.chips(5);hud();}
$('chips').addEventListener('click',function(e){var c=e.target.closest('.chip');if(!c||c.disabled)return;chipV=+c.getAttribute('data-v');
  if(target)addBet(target,chipV,c);else{setButtons();msg('Chip '+money(chipV)+' selected. Tap <b>Player</b>, <b>Banker</b>, <b>Tie</b> or a side bet to place it.');}});
$('layout').addEventListener('click',function(e){var s=e.target.closest('[data-spot]');if(!s)return;if(Tip.longPressed())return;var id=s.getAttribute('data-spot');
  if(phase!=='bet'||busy||liveClosed())return;target=id;var ch=document.querySelector('#chips .chip[data-v="'+chipV+'"]');if(bank<chipV){var cs=[5000,1000,500,100,25,5].filter(function(v){return v<=bank;});if(cs.length)chipV=cs[0];}
  addBet(id,chipV,ch&&ch.offsetWidth?ch:s);});
$('layout').addEventListener('contextmenu',function(e){var s=e.target.closest('[data-spot]');if(!s)return;e.preventDefault();var id=s.getAttribute('data-spot');if(phase!=='bet'||busy||!bets[id])return;bank+=bets[id];delete bets[id];SFX.chip();hud();});
$('clearBtn').addEventListener('click',clearBets);
$('rebetBtn').addEventListener('click',rebet);
$('dblBtn').addEventListener('click',doubleBets);
$('dealBtn').addEventListener('click',function(){deal();});
$('rebuyBtn').addEventListener('click',function(){bank+=1000;start+=1000;toast('Rebought $1,000');hud();});
$('roadsBtn').addEventListener('click',function(e){e.stopPropagation();var r=$('roads');r.classList.toggle('pop');if(r.classList.contains('pop'))setTimeout(drawRoads,0);});
document.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(e){var r=$('roads');if(r.classList.contains('pop')&&!r.contains(e.target)&&!(e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#roadsBtn')))r.classList.remove('pop');});
document.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){
  if(e.target&&(e.target.tagName==='INPUT'||e.target.tagName==='TEXTAREA'))return;
  if(Squeeze.active()||Ceremony.active())return;
  var k=e.key.toLowerCase();
  if(k==='enter'){if(phase==='bet'&&!busy){e.preventDefault();deal();}}
  else if(k==='r')rebet();else if(k==='x')clearBets();else if(k==='2')doubleBets();
});

/* ---------- options ---------- */
function seg(id,fn){[].forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#'+id+' button'),function(b){
  b.addEventListener('click',function(){[].forEach.call(b.parentNode.children,function(x){x.classList.toggle('on',x===b);});fn(b.getAttribute('data-v'));});});}
function applyRuleUI(){rulesText();if(enforceSides())hud();renderSideMenu();renderSpots();Tip.refresh();}
function ruleChange(k,v){
  if(MODE==='view'){toast('The host sets the table rules while you play live');LV.applyRules();return;}
  if(phase!=='bet'||busy){pendingR=pendingR||{};pendingR[k]=v;toast('Rule change starts with the next coup');return;}
  R[k]=v;applyRuleUI();
}
seg('optComm',function(v){ruleChange('comm',v);});
seg('optTie',function(v){ruleChange('tie',+v);});
seg('optDeal',function(v){R.cine=v==='1';});

seg('optShuf',function(v){R.shufCine=v==='1';});
seg('optSound',function(v){soundOn=v==='1';});
seg('optBots',function(v){BOTSN=+v;if(MODE!=='solo'){toast('Computer players sit out while others are here');return;}if(phase==='bet'&&!busy){for(var i=seats.length-1;i>=BOTSN;i--){var s=seats[i];for(var k in s.bets){s.bank+=s.bets[k];}s.bets={};}syncBots(false);botsBet(R.cine);}else toast('Seats change after this coup');});
seg('optTalk',function(v){TALK=v==='1';if(!TALK)document.querySelectorAll('.talk.on').forEach(function(t){t.classList.remove('on');});});

/* the option buttons are remembered by the arcade; the side-bet picks and last bets here */
var XKEY='su_arcade_baccarat_x';
function saveExtra(){try{localStorage.setItem(XKEY,JSON.stringify({sides:activeSides,last:lastBets,chip:chipV}));}catch(e){}}
function loadExtra(){try{var o=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(XKEY)||'null');if(!o)return;
  if(Array.isArray(o.sides))activeSides=o.sides.filter(function(id){return !!sideDef(id);}).slice(0,3);
  if(o.last&&typeof o.last==='object'){lastBets={};for(var k in o.last){var v=+o.last[k];if(v>0&&v<1e7)lastBets[k]=v;}}
  if([5,25,100,500,1000,5000].indexOf(o.chip)>=0)chipV=o.chip;}catch(e){}}

/* ---------- layout fitting ---------- */
/* app layout: on tall phones the scoreboard sits on the table between the cards and the betting grid (like live-baccarat apps); elsewhere it is its own panel */
function placeRoads(){var r=$('roads'),bt=$('bt'),felt=$('felt'),lay=$('layout');if(!r||!bt||!lay)return;
  if(document.documentElement.classList.contains('fsnarrow')){if(r.parentNode!==bt){r.classList.remove('pop');bt.insertBefore(r,lay);}}
  else if(r.parentNode!==felt){felt.insertBefore(r,bt);}}
function fit(){placeRoads();var w=$('felt').clientWidth;$('felt').classList.toggle('small',w<600);drawRoads();fitFs();}
/* full screen: shrink the cards until the whole table fits the screen */
function fitFs(){
  var H=document.documentElement,felt=$('felt'),bt=$('bt');felt.style.removeProperty('--cw');
  if(!H.classList.contains('fs'))return;
  var cw=$('sP1').offsetWidth;
  var tr=bt.querySelector('.boxes');function over(){return bt.scrollHeight>bt.clientHeight+1||tr.scrollWidth>tr.clientWidth+1;}
  for(var i=0;i<60&&over()&&cw>24;i++){cw-=2;felt.style.setProperty('--cw',cw+'px');}
}
var fitT=0;function fitSoon(){clearTimeout(fitT);fitT=setTimeout(fit,60);}
addEventListener('resize',fitSoon);if(window.ResizeObserver){try{new ResizeObserver(fitSoon).observe($('felt'));}catch(e){}}

/* ---------- table chat: the shared panel, with computer players answering ---------- */
var L=Live.create({game:'baccarat',maxSeats:5});
CH=Live.chat(L,document.getElementById('felt'),{local:function(){return botsSeated()>0;},localLabel:'Computer players',myName:function(){return 'You';},
  onMessage:function(m){if(!TALK)return;Live.banter(m.text,botList(),{game:'baccarat',
    advice:function(){var lw=lastNonTie(),st=streakLen();return 'Banker has the lowest edge, '+bankerEdge().toFixed(2)+'%. Skip the Tie unless it pays 9 to 1.'+(st>=3?' And the road shows '+(lw==='B'?'Banker':'Player')+' '+st+' in a row.':'');},
    mood:function(b){var s=seatById(b.id);if(!s)return 'even';return s.bank>s.start*1.05?'up':s.bank<s.start*0.95?'down':'even';},
    say:function(b,txt,delay){var s=seatById(b.id);if(s)say(s,txt,delay);}});}});

/* ---------- live table: people on the site at the same time share one coup ----------
   The oldest person at the table is the host: its browser runs the betting clock and the shoe,
   and broadcasts each coup's cards. Everyone animates the same coup and settles only their own bets. */
var BigTimer=(function(){
  var until=0,total=15000,t=0,sub='';
  function draw(){var el=$('readyBig');if(!el)return;var left=until-Date.now();
    if(left<=0||paused){el.classList.remove('on');if(left<=0){clearInterval(t);t=0;}return;}
    el.classList.add('on');var sec=Math.ceil(left/1000);$('readyNum').textContent=sec;el.classList.toggle('urgent',sec<=3);
    el.querySelector('.rb').style.setProperty('--p',Math.max(0,Math.min(1,left/total)).toFixed(3));$('readySub').textContent=sub;}
  return {set:function(ms,s){ms=Math.max(0,ms||0);if(s!==undefined)sub=s;
    if(ms<=0){until=0;draw();return;}
    var nu=Date.now()+ms;if(Math.abs(nu-until)>400)until=nu;
    if(!t)t=setInterval(draw,200);draw();}};
})();
var LV=(function(){
  var enabled=true,away=false,settled=false,remote={},lastState=null,cur=null,lastHost=null,lastSeq=-1,rcvAt=0;
  var myRound=0,dealtRound=-1,want=false,betsT=0,soloRules=null,H=null,hostT=0,seq=0,lastCast=0,retryT=0;
  function hue(id){var h=0;for(var i=0;i<id.length;i++)h=(h*31+id.charCodeAt(i))>>>0;return 'hsl('+(h%360)+',75%,62%)';}
  function sum(o){var t=0;for(var k in o)t+=+o[k]||0;return t;}
  function others(){return L.roster().filter(function(p){return !p.isMe;});}
  function hostId(){var h=L.roster().filter(function(p){return p.isHost;})[0];return h?h.id:null;}
  /* ---- seats: other humans fill the computer players' chairs ---- */
  function liveSeats(){
    var os=others().slice(0,4),ids={};os.forEach(function(p){ids['h:'+p.id]=1;});
    seats=seats.filter(function(s){if(s.human&&ids[s.id])return true;if(s.el)s.el.remove();return false;});
    os.forEach(function(p){var s=seatById('h:'+p.id);
      if(!s){s={id:'h:'+p.id,key:p.id,name:p.name,col:hue(p.id),sty:'human',slot:freeSlot(),bank:0,start:0,bets:{},hands:0,lastNet:0,el:null,human:true};seats.push(s);}
      s.name=p.name;var r=remote[p.id];if(r){s.bank=r.bank||0;if(phase==='bet'&&!busy)s.bets=(r.round===myRound)?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(r.bets||{})):{};}});
    renderSeats();renderSpots();
  }
  function botsLeave(){
    var bots=seats.filter(function(s){return !s.human;});
    bots.forEach(function(s,i){for(var k in s.bets)s.bank+=s.bets[k];s.bets={};
      chatPost(s,pick(['Looks like a crowd. I will step away for a bit.','Real players are here. I will grab a drink.','Your table now, folks. Good luck.']));
      if(s.el){s.el.classList.add('leave');(function(el){setTimeout(function(){el.remove();},450);})(s.el);}});
    seats=seats.filter(function(s){return s.human;});
  }
  function enterLive(){
    botsLeave();soloRules={comm:R.comm,tie:R.tie};lastBets=lastBets||{};
    ['optComm','optTie','optBots'].forEach(function(id){var o=$(id);if(o)o.closest('.opt').classList.add('dis');});
    msg('You joined a <b>live table</b>. Place your bets before the timer runs out.');
  }
  function leaveLive(){
    stopHost();BigTimer.set(0);
    /* bets that never reached a result come back */
    if(betTotal()>0&&phase==='bet'&&!busy){clearBets(true);toast('Live round cancelled: your bets came back');}
    seats.filter(function(s){return s.human;}).forEach(function(s){if(s.el)s.el.remove();});
    seats=seats.filter(function(s){return !s.human;});
    if(soloRules){R.comm=soloRules.comm;R.tie=soloRules.tie;soloRules=null;applyRuleUI();}
    ['optComm','optTie','optBots'].forEach(function(id){var o=$(id);if(o)o.closest('.opt').classList.remove('dis');});
    remote={};cur=null;want=false;
    syncBots(false);botsBet(R.cine);hud();
  }
  function decide(){
    clearTimeout(retryT);
    var live=enabled&&L.available()&&L.status==='live'&&L.roster().length>=2&&!away&&settled;
    var m=!live?'solo':(L.isHost()?'host':'view');
    if(m===MODE){if(m!=='solo')liveSeats();liveUI();return;}
    if(MODE==='host'&&m==='view'){MODE='view';stopHost();liveUI();return;}
    if(phase!=='bet'||busy||Ceremony.active()||Squeeze.active()){retryT=setTimeout(decide,600);return;}
    var prev=MODE;MODE=m;
    if(prev==='solo')enterLive();
    if(m==='solo')leaveLive();
    else if(m==='host')startHost();
    else{L.send('hello',{});liveSeats();}
    liveUI();hud();
  }
  /* ---- host engine ---- */
  function stopHost(){clearInterval(hostT);hostT=0;H=null;}
  function startHost(){
    var ls=lastState,now=Date.now();
    H={round:1,phase:'bet',closesAt:now+15000,ready:{},want:{},result:null,burn:null,histBefore:null,resultAt:0};
    if(ls){H.round=(ls.phase==='result'&&dealtRound===ls.round)?ls.round+1:ls.round;
      if(Math.abs(shoe.length-(ls.shoeLeft||0))>0&&totalCards)needShuffle=true;}
    if(!totalCards)needShuffle=true;
    seq=Math.max(seq,lastSeq)+1000;
    hostT=setInterval(hostTick,250);cast();
  }
  function stateObj(){var now=Date.now();
    return {phase:H.phase,round:H.round,seq:++seq,hostId:L.me.id,closesAt:Math.max(0,H.closesAt-now),rules:{comm:R.comm,tie:R.tie},
      shoeLeft:shoe.length,cutAt:cutAt,needShuffle:needShuffle,history:H.result?H.histBefore:history.slice(),result:H.result,burn:H.burn};}
  function cast(to){if(!H)return;var st=stateObj();L.send('state',st,to);lastCast=Date.now();if(!to)onState(st,true);}
  function humansWithBets(){return L.roster().filter(function(p){if(p.isMe)return betTotal()>0;var r=remote[p.id];return r&&r.round===H.round&&sum(r.bets)>0;});}
  function hostTick(){
    if(MODE!=='host'||!H||paused)return;var now=Date.now();
    if(H.phase==='bet'){
      var wb=humansWithBets();
      if(wb.length&&wb.every(function(p){return H.want[p.id];})&&H.closesAt-now>2000){H.closesAt=now+2000;cast();}
      if(now>=H.closesAt){
        if(!wb.length){H.closesAt=now+15000;cast();return;}
        H.phase='locked';cast();setTimeout(hostResult,1000);return;}
    } else if(H.phase==='result'){
      var ids=L.roster().map(function(p){return p.id;});
      if(ids.every(function(id){return H.ready[id];})||now-H.resultAt>25000){
        H.round++;H.phase='bet';H.closesAt=now+15000;H.ready={};H.want={};H.result=null;H.burn=null;cast();return;}
    }
    if(now-lastCast>2000)cast();
  }
  function hostResult(){
    if(MODE!=='host'||!H||H.phase!=='locked')return;
    H.histBefore=history.slice();H.burn=null;
    if(needShuffle||!totalCards){var b=buildShoeQuick();H.burn={card:{r:b.card.r,s:b.card.s},n:b.n};H.histBefore=[];}
    var c=[drawCard(),drawCard(),drawCard(),drawCard()],p=[c[0],c[2]],bb=[c[1],c[3]],pt=tot(p),bt=tot(bb);
    if(pt<8&&bt<8){var p3=null;if(pt<=5){var x=drawCard();c.push(x);p.push(x);p3=bv(x);}if(bankerDraws(bt,p3)){var y=drawCard();c.push(y);bb.push(y);}}
    var X=coupCtx(p,bb);
    H.result={cards:c.map(function(k){return {r:k.r,s:k.s};}),w:X.w,pt:X.pt,bt:X.bt};
    H.phase='result';H.resultAt=Date.now();H.ready={};cast();
  }
  /* ---- every client (the host included) follows the state ---- */
  function onState(st,self){
    if(!self){if(st.hostId===lastHost&&st.seq<=lastSeq)return;lastHost=st.hostId;lastSeq=st.seq;rcvAt=Date.now();}
    lastState=st;
    if(MODE==='solo'||(MODE==='host'&&!self))return;
    cur=st;
    if(MODE==='view')applyRules();
    if(st.round!==myRound&&st.phase==='bet'){myRound=st.round;want=false;sendBets();}
    if(st.phase==='bet'){var n=L.roster().length,rd=0;L.roster().forEach(function(p){if(p.isMe?want:(H?H.want[p.id]:false))rd++;});
      BigTimer.set(st.closesAt,MODE==='host'?rd+' of '+n+' players ready':n+' players at the table');
      if(!busy&&phase==='bet'&&dealtRound!==st.round&&!/live table/.test($('msg').innerHTML)&&st.closesAt>13500)msg('Place your bets. <b>Betting closes in '+Math.ceil(st.closesAt/1000)+'s</b>.');}
    else BigTimer.set(0);
    if(st.phase==='locked'&&!busy){msg('<b>No more bets.</b>');caption('No more bets',1600);}
    if(st.phase==='result'&&st.result&&dealtRound!==st.round){
      if(phase!=='bet'||busy){setTimeout(function(){if(lastState===st||cur===st)onState(st,true);},500);return;}
      dealtRound=st.round;myRound=st.round;startCoup(st);
    }
    setButtons();liveUI();
  }
  function startCoup(st){
    var cs=st.result.cards.map(function(c){return {r:c.r,s:c.s,id:nextId++};}),k=cs.length;
    if(MODE==='host'){for(var i=k-1;i>=0;i--)shoe.push(cs[i]);}
    else{
      if(st.burn||!totalCards){prepareShoe();if(st.burn){shoesPlayed++;toast('Fresh shoe \u00b7 burn card '+RANK_TXT[st.burn.card.r]+', '+st.burn.n+' burned');}discards=st.burn?1+st.burn.n:0;}
      history=(st.history||[]).slice();drawRoads();
      while(shoe.length>st.shoeLeft+k&&shoe.length)shoe.shift();
      shoe.splice(Math.max(0,shoe.length-k),k);for(var j=k-1;j>=0;j--)shoe.push(cs[j]);
      cutAt=st.cutAt;cutReached=shoe.length<=cutAt;needShuffle=false;shoeHud();
    }
    if(st.burn&&MODE==='host'){shoesPlayed++;toast('Fresh shoe \u00b7 burn card '+RANK_TXT[st.burn.card.r]+', '+st.burn.n+' burned');history=[];drawRoads();}
    BigTimer.set(0);
    deal(cs);
  }
  function applyRules(){if(!cur||!cur.rules)return;var ch=false;['comm','tie'].forEach(function(k){if(R[k]!==cur.rules[k]){R[k]=cur.rules[k];ch=true;}});
    if(ch){applyRuleUI();[['optComm',R.comm],['optTie',String(R.tie)]].forEach(function(a){[].forEach.call($(a[0]).children,function(b){b.classList.toggle('on',b.getAttribute('data-v')===String(a[1]));});});}}
  function sendBets(to){if(MODE==='solo')return;L.send('bets',{round:myRound,bets:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(bets)),name:L.me.name,bank:bank},to);}
  function betsSoon(){clearTimeout(betsT);betsT=setTimeout(sendBets,250);}
  function wantDeal(){want=true;if(MODE==='host'&&H)H.want[L.me.id]=1;else L.send('ready',{round:myRound,wantDeal:true},hostId());toast('Ready. The coup is dealt when everyone is ready or the timer runs out.');setButtons();}
  function done(){if(MODE==='host'&&H){if(H.round===dealtRound)H.ready[L.me.id]=1;}else L.send('ready',{round:dealtRound,done:true},hostId());}
  function liveUI(){
    var s=L.status,dot=$('liveDot'),txt=$('liveTxt'),avail=L.available();
    dot.className='ldot '+(!avail||!enabled?'off':s);
    txt.textContent=!avail?'Offline (live tables are not set up)':!enabled?'Off: you are playing alone':s==='live'?'Live at Table '+L.table:s==='connecting'?'Connecting\u2026':s==='error'?'Could not connect. Playing alone.':'Offline';
    var r=L.roster();
    $('livePeople').innerHTML=r.length?r.map(function(p){return '<span class="lp'+(p.isMe?' me':'')+'">'+esc(p.name)+(p.isHost?' <i>host</i>':'')+(p.isMe?' <i>you</i>':'')+'</span>';}).join(''):'<span class="lp">Nobody else here right now. Anyone who opens Baccarat on this site sits down with you.</span>';
    var n=r.length;$('liveChip').textContent='LIVE \u00b7 '+n+' players';$('liveChip').classList.toggle('hide',MODE==='solo'||n<2);
    var bw=$('botWho');if(MODE!=='solo')bw.innerHTML='<div>Computer players sit out while others are here.</div>';else botWho();
    try{CH.refresh();}catch(e){}
    if(document.activeElement!==$('liveName'))$('liveName').value=L.me.name;
  }
  L.on('status',function(s){if(s==='live'&&!settled)setTimeout(function(){settled=true;decide();},900);if(s!=='live')settled=false;decide();});
  L.on('roster',function(r){var ids={};r.forEach(function(p){ids[p.id]=1;});for(var k in remote)if(!ids[k])delete remote[k];if(H)for(var k2 in H.ready)if(!ids[k2])delete H.ready[k2];decide();});
  L.on('host',function(){decide();});
  L.on('msg:state',function(st){onState(st,false);});
  L.on('msg:bets',function(p,from){if(!p)return;if(MODE!=='solo'&&cur&&p.round===cur.round&&cur.phase!=='bet'&&remote[from]&&remote[from].round===p.round)return;
    remote[from]={round:p.round,bets:p.bets||{},name:String(p.name||'Guest').slice(0,18),bank:+p.bank||0};if(MODE!=='solo')liveSeats();});
  L.on('msg:ready',function(p,from){if(MODE!=='host'||!H||!p)return;if(p.round!==H.round)return;if(p.wantDeal)H.want[from]=1;if(p.done)H.ready[from]=1;});
  L.on('msg:hello',function(p,from){if(MODE==='solo')return;if(MODE==='host')cast(from);sendBets(from);});
  addEventListener('message',function(e){var d=e.data;if(!d||d.arcade!=='visible')return;away=!d.on;setTimeout(decide,40);});
  seg('optLive',function(v){enabled=v==='1';if(enabled)L.start();else L.stop();decide();});
  $('liveName').addEventListener('change',function(){L.setName($('liveName').value);liveUI();sendBets();});
  return {closed:function(){return !cur||cur.phase!=='bet'||dealtRound===cur.round;},betsSoon:betsSoon,wantDeal:wantDeal,wanted:function(){return want;},done:done,applyRules:applyRules,
    start:function(){if(enabled)L.start();liveUI();},
    info:function(){return {mode:MODE,round:myRound,dealtRound:dealtRound,phase:cur?cur.phase:null,hostId:hostId(),status:L.status,roster:L.roster().length,remote:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(remote)),host:H?{round:H.round,phase:H.phase}:null};},
    shortWindow:function(ms){if(H){H.closesAt=Date.now()+ms;cast();}}};
})();

function parseCard(t){if(typeof t==='object')return {r:t.r,s:t.s||'s',id:nextId++};var m=String(t).match(/^(10|[2-9AJQKT])([shdc])?$/i);if(!m)throw new Error('bad card '+t);
  var rk={A:1,J:11,Q:12,K:13,T:10}[m[1].toUpperCase()]||+m[1];return {r:rk,s:(m[2]||'s').toLowerCase(),id:nextId++};}
window.__baccarat_dbg={
  /* put cards on top of the shoe: the first one listed is dealt first (P1, B1, P2, B2, then third cards) */
  stack:function(list){if(needShuffle&&!totalCards){buildShoeQuick();}needShuffle=false;var cs=list.map(parseCard);for(var i=cs.length-1;i>=0;i--)shoe.push(cs[i]);if(cutAt>shoe.length-10)cutAt=0;shoeHud();return shoe.length;},
  readyShoe:function(){if(!totalCards||needShuffle){buildShoeQuick();}return shoe.length;},
  state:function(){return {phase:phase,busy:busy,bank:bank,start:start,bets:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(bets)),player:hand.p.map(function(c){return RANK_TXT[c.r]+c.s;}),banker:hand.b.map(function(c){return RANK_TXT[c.r]+c.s;}),
    history:history.slice(),shoeLeft:shoe.length,cutAt:cutAt,discards:discards,needShuffle:needShuffle,rules:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(R)),activeSides:activeSides.slice(),squeezing:Squeeze.active(),shuffling:Ceremony.active(),paused:paused,timers:timers.length,
    msg:$('msg').innerText,caption:$('capT').textContent,stats:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(ST)),
    bots:seats.map(function(s){return {id:s.id,name:s.name,sty:s.sty,bank:s.bank,bets:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(s.bets)),slot:s.slot};})};},
  bet:function(id,amt){return addBet(id,amt,null);},
  clear:clearBets,deal:function(){return deal();},
  sides:function(list){activeSides=list.slice(0,3);renderSideMenu();hud();},
  coup:function(list){var x=playCoup(list.map(parseCard));return {w:x.w,pt:x.pt,bt:x.bt,p:x.p.map(function(c){return RANK_TXT[c.r];}),b:x.b.map(function(c){return RANK_TXT[c.r];}),n:x.n};},
  settle:function(id,amt,list){var x=playCoup(list.map(parseCard));return settleBet(id,amt,x);},
  setHistory:function(h){history=h.map(function(w){return typeof w==='string'?{w:w,pt:w==='P'?7:5,bt:w==='B'?7:5,pp:false,bp:false,nat:false}:w;});drawRoads();},
  roads:function(){var B=bigRoadOf(history);return {cols:B.cols,cells:B.cells.map(function(c){return [c.c,c.r,c.h.w];}),eye:derivedMarks(B.cols,1),small:derivedMarks(B.cols,2),roach:derivedMarks(B.cols,3)};},
  bots:function(n){BOTSN=n;syncBots(false);},
  bankroll:function(v){bank=v;hud();},
  botBank:function(i,v){if(seats[i]){seats[i].bank=v;updateSeat(seats[i]);}},
  skipShuffle:function(){Ceremony.skip();},
  squeezeAll:function(){Squeeze.skipAll();},
  rule:function(k,v){R[k]=v;applyRuleUI();},
  pause:pauseAll,resume:resumeAll,
  live:function(){return LV.info();},shortWindow:function(ms){LV.shortWindow(ms);}
};
})();

<\u002fscript>
<script>
(function(){
  var last=0;
  var sent=document.createElement('div');
  sent.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
  sent.style.cssText='height:0;margin:0;padding:0;border:0;clear:both';
  function measure(){
    if(!document.body) return 0;
    if(sent.parentNode!==document.body) document.body.appendChild(sent);
    var pb=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.body).paddingBottom)||0;
    return Math.ceil(sent.getBoundingClientRect().bottom+(window.pageYOffset||0)+pb);
  }
  function post(){
    var h=measure();
    if(!h||Math.abs(h-last)<2) return;
    last=h;
    try{parent.postMessage({arcade:'h',h:h},'*');}catch(e){}
  }
  addEventListener('load',post); addEventListener('resize',post);
  if(window.ResizeObserver){
    try{ var ro=new ResizeObserver(post); ro.observe(document.body);
         var w=document.querySelector('.wrap'); if(w) ro.observe(w); }catch(e){}
  }
  document.addEventListener('load',post,true);
  addEventListener('message',function(e){if(e.data&&e.data.arcade==='visible'&&e.data.on){last=0;setTimeout(post,30);}});
  setInterval(post,900); post();
})();
<\u002fscript>
